## CURRENT — encryption/export compliance preflight and proposed iOS declaration (November 24, 2026)

**Baseline:** ios-prep at 48b1b2a94ba61cdce38b3af7ab424c5c032dc647 after merged [PR #214](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/214).

- [x] **Scoped encryption source/dependency review:** inspected three first-party Swift runtime files, package.json and lockfile (runtime entries Capacitor Core 8.5.2, Capacitor iOS 8.5.2, tslib 2.8.1), native SPM package, tracked Capacitor configuration, and 16 selected JS/HTML files covering profile/backup/gate/bridge, service worker, Flashcards/SoundTouchJS and Ghost Writer. No app-provided confidentiality encryption implementation identified in these files. App/profile crypto.randomUUID creates local IDs; parental gate crypto.getRandomValues randomizes arithmetic questions. Family backup export uses JSON.stringify and application/json; it is NOT a password-encrypted backup. Base64 media encoding is not encryption. Upstream Capacitor 8.5.2 AppUUID.swift invokes Apple CommonCrypto SHA-256 to hash a random identifier, not to encrypt user content; reviewed HTTP paths use OS URLSession. OS HTTPS, data protection, hashing/randomness and signing are distinct from implementing non-exempt confidentiality encryption.
- **Proposed release metadata:** add boolean false for ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption in ios/App/App/Info.plist, referenced by both Xcode build configurations. Per [Apple's key documentation](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/bundleresources/information-property-list/itsappusesnonexemptencryption) this means no encryption or only exempt encryption, NOT an assertion that no cryptography exists. [Apple export compliance overview](https://developer.apple.com/help/app-store-connect/manage-app-information/overview-of-export-compliance) and [documentation matrix](https://developer.apple.com/help/app-store-connect/reference/app-information/export-compliance-documentation-for-encryption) support the proposed answer if the final app is limited to OS encryption. Do not provide an invented compliance code.
- [ ] **Owner review and final verification:** whole-repo source scan was not completed (connector call limit); the scoped finding is not exhaustive or a binary classification. Before final release, review remaining tracked/packaged runtime code and any unexpected cryptography hits, check built Release archive's Info.plist boolean and dependency inventory, and reconcile actual App Store Connect questionnaire/storefronts. If findings differ, revise the declaration. No build or archive run in this checkpoint.
- [ ] **Separate export-law/storefront determination:** Apple documentation exemption does not establish exemption from all U.S. or destination-country obligations; review applicable classification/reporting and France requirements before distribution. See [BIS encryption controls](https://www.bis.gov/learn-support/encryption-controls) and [Apple compliance guidance](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/security/complying-with-encryption-export-regulations). No ECCN, CCATS, or annual reporting exemption claimed. Seek qualified export guidance if needed.
- Scope: proposed release metadata + handoff only; no gameplay, dependency, storage, assets, signing, or main changes. Owner reviews/merges. Continue pre-enrollment roadmap; do not enroll yet.

## CURRENT — scoped SDK runtime privacy assessment complete; owner policy approval next (November 24, 2026)

**Source baseline:** ios-prep at 446b739d4f6e4ba8575a84d0a5887fa670be08cf following merged [PR #211](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/211).

- [x] **Scoped SDK runtime privacy assessment:** reviewed selected upstream [Capacitor 8.5.2 iOS source](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor/tree/8.5.2/ios), including CAPBridgeViewController, CAPInstanceDescriptor, CapacitorBridge, WebViewAssetHandler, CapacitorHttp, HttpRequestHandler, CapacitorCookies, AppUUID, KeyValueStore, Cordova CDVURLProtocol and both privacy manifests. BibloZoo's tracked capacitor.config.json specifies dist and has no remote server URL or HTTP interception enablement. WebViewAssetHandler serves local assets and gates its HTTP interceptor on explicit enablement (default false); direct CapacitorHttp bridge calls can still perform caller-specified network requests via URLSession. AppUUID can generate a local hashed random UUID, and KeyValueStore persists local files rather than UserDefaults; reviewed paths did not identify automatic analytics or transmission of that identifier. Upstream manifests agree with prior archive declarations (no collected data or tracking). These targeted findings complement the first-party audit, SoundTouchJS review, simulator proxy and physical iPhone report; not exhaustive source-to-binary equivalence, all SDK paths, network capture or required-reason API verification.
- **Proposed App Privacy answer:** No data collected for the packaged native app under [Apple's definition](https://developer.apple.com/app-store/app-privacy-details/), pending owner approval and final production archive/upload checks. User-directed OS sharing and website hosting are distinct from automatic developer/SDK collection. If final evidence differs, revisit the answer.
- **Policy review:** read actual ios-prep privacy_policy.html and main app-store-privacy.html; substantive visible wording matches, both dated November 22, 2026. Owner approval of wording and contact mailbox remains OPEN. No policy changes in this checkpoint. Potential clarifications for owner review: saved/shared copies outside app control and OS backup/sync may persist independently of app deletion; app permissions can be changed in Settings. Do not assert these OS settings were tested.
- **Next:** owner policy/contact and proposed disclosure approval; final archive and upload validation remain separate. No code, data, dependency, signing or licensing changes.

## HISTORICAL — Kids Category parental-gate source/policy review; retain current 1.0 math candidate (November 23, 2026 ET)

**Current verified native baseline:** `ios-prep` at `42b8d6124549326cb8128cf85773b3b79672a4fb`, after merged [PR #207](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/207) records the real-device family backup v3 round-trip PASS. No change to the live gameplay or math code is proposed by this checkpoint.

**Apple policy check (current guidance, November 23, 2026):** [App Review Guidelines 1.3](https://developer.apple.com/app-store/review/guidelines/#kids-category) restrict outside links/purchasing/distractions behind a parental gate. Apple's [Kids design page](https://developer.apple.com/kids/) calls these **adult-level tasks** and gives illustrations including sequence taps, moving a geometric shape, and an adult-knowledge question. These are **illustrations, not an Apple-approved minimum test**; Apple does **not** specify numeric operands or formally approve BibloZoo's challenge. [Guideline 5.1.4](https://developer.apple.com/app-store/review/guidelines/#kids) separately addresses child privacy; a gate is not legal parental consent.

**Actual source reviewed:** `verse_games/shared/parental-gate.js` uses cryptographically random `A − B + C`, A=20..99, B=10..A−1, C=1..9, answer 2..98, with no automatic approval, up to 3 wrong attempts, a fresh question after each wrong entry, one-shot action callback, Cancel/Not Now, fail-closed missing native identity/randomness/background, and no permanent unlock or stored answer. This is a **two-step calculation**, more demanding than some illustrative Apple example screens, but some proficient 6–8-year-olds could still answer (e.g. 20 − 19 + 1 = 2). This is a **reasonable 1.0 candidate**, **not proved to meet Apple's adult-level criterion**.

**Recommendation (awaiting owner's product sign-off):** **Keep the existing Grown-up Check for version 1.0**, avoid speculative difficulty increases or redesign after the owner's verified iPhone VoiceOver and iPad keyboard acceptance, and clearly explain protected actions and the randomized check in App Review notes. Owner/Apple suitability decision remains separate and **OPEN** until signed off; if Apple objects or an actual too-easy bypass is observed, propose a narrowly scoped math-strengthening PR then. Do not label the gate approved by Apple before review.

**Next focused release work:** Resolve [asset/font/audio/Scripture rights inventory](ASSET-LICENSES.md) and App Privacy/SDK disclosures before final App Store Connect Kids band decision (proposed Education / Made for Kids ages 6–8), screenshots, archive/TestFlight. Keep `main` PWA and Audio Test distinct; no family data handling, installs or gameplay changes needed for this policy-review documentation PR. Per owner preference, log each checked-off source review or observed result in a small owner-reviewed PR; never merge automatically.

## HISTORICAL — owner decision: Larger Text deferred for BibloZoo 1.0; iPad portrait check next (November 23, 2026 ET)

**Owner-approved version 1.0 scope decision:** After actual iPhone testing showed that enabling iOS **Larger Accessibility Sizes** did **not** visibly resize BibloZoo text, the owner accepted the recommendation to **keep the existing carefully fitted game text/layouts for 1.0**. Do **not** implement a broad WebView/Dynamic Type resizing system, redesign verse/game containers or claim **Larger Text** support in App Store accessibility information for this version. Treat comprehensive system-driven text scaling as a possible **post-1.0 enhancement**, not a presumed Kids Category-specific obligation or automatic App Review blocker. This is a product scope decision, **not a successful Larger Text test**.

**Practical exception:** Any **observed severe clipping or unreadable essential control**, especially Grown-up Check, Cancel, backup/privacy management, should receive a targeted fix regardless of the deferred global feature. Do not mark full accessibility acceptance passed. VoiceOver initial announcement/swipe behavior remains the narrow owner-reported PASS from merged [#201](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/201) and the merged documentation checkpoint [#202](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/202); modal containment and full screen reader matrix are separate.

**iPad portrait owner observation — partial PASS / keyboard usability issue open:** The owner tested the native Grown-up Check on an iPad and reports the popup works well in portrait overall. After tapping the Answer field, however, the iPad software keyboard **does not move the popup upward as it does on the tested iPhone**. The Answer field stays visible, but the **Check Answer action is behind the keyboard; the owner must dismiss the keyboard to reach the button**. Record layout and basic popup presentation as **PASS for observed non-keyboard behavior**, and **keyboard-visible Check Answer accessibility as NOT PASS / focused fix candidate**. Do not claim complete iPad acceptance; the owner did not report a separate iPad VoiceOver or keyboard Return-key test. No actual backup export/import/data reset was requested or performed as part of this test.

**Next focused action:** Investigate a **modal-only** iPad software-keyboard viewport adjustment or equivalent reachable submit action, preserving existing iPhone behavior, math, cancel and the no-automatic-keyboard fix. A possible cause is the fixed-position Capacitor WKWebView overlay and an on-screen keyboard reducing the *visual* viewport without changing the CSS layout viewport; this is an informed hypothesis, **not a diagnosed device root cause**. Use a separately reviewed small code PR and test on real iPad before checking off keyboard-visible usability. Do not redesign games or implement full Dynamic Type. Do not test unsupported iPad landscape. Then proceed to native bridge/Kids/privacy and synthetic v3 restore sign-offs. Preserve real family/Audio Test profiles; keep `main` separate from `ios-prep`.

## HISTORICAL — Zoo family and individual backup v3 completeness candidate (November 23, 2026)

**Scope:** A read-only audit of the owner's two private November 23 backups (one family, one individual) confirmed both use v2 progress-only exports. The family file contains 11 Zookeeper entries and the individual is one of those profiles with newer Flashcards progress. Do **not** commit either real JSON file, names, profile IDs, or progress into GitHub or tests. The separate active localStorage records `biblozooZooEscapeStats:v1:<id>` (four rescue/pending-celebration counters) and `biblozooZooSurprise:v2:<id>` (today's break odds/history) are omitted by both v2 exporters. Current installed data has **not** been erased, migrated, or modified.

**Candidate fix:** `fix/ios-zoo-family-profile-backup-v3-20261123` → `ios-prep` (owner-review PR, do not auto-merge). Both Family and Individual backups now emit **saveVersion 3** with a per-profile `zoo` object containing validated `rescueStats` and `surprise` records; explicit `null` means that a corresponding independent storage key did not exist. The shared `verse_games/shared/zoo-backup-state.js` reads/writes only those two known per-Zookeeper prefixes and fails closed if an existing record is corrupt, not quietly zeroing achievements. Family/individual import accepts **both v2 and v3**. V3 Add remaps Zoo state to the new generated profile ID, v3 Replace Progress writes to the selected active profile, and v3 Replace All removes orphaned Zoo records. For v2, missing Zoo state cannot be recovered; a matching live local ID retains its existing independent counters rather than being overwritten with fabricated zeros. Import rollback snapshots and restores the independent records as well as the main profile registry/progress. Other localStorage keys are untouched.

**Checks:** New synthetic `scripts/validate_zoo_backup_integrity.js` uses the actual app export, parse, and import functions under fictional storage to test both formats, remapping, Replace All cleanup, old-v2 preservation, corruption, staged storage failure and rollback, and source/packaged inclusion. Existing read-only offline `scripts/validate_family_backup_file.js` accepts v2 and v3, validates new Zoo state shape, and never prints private names/IDs. Both source and packaged jobs run the new suite. GitHub CI and real app export/import acceptance must be verified independently. No actual user backup or live family records were imported or edited; `main`, Audio Test and signing remain separate.

**Owner acceptance after optional merge:** Keep all live app profiles installed. Export a fresh **family backup and individual backup** from updated production BibloZoo, and submit copies privately for read-only shape comparison. Expect `saveVersion: 3` and `zoo.rescueStats` / `zoo.surprise` per Zookeeper (null only if that record never existed). For actual restore tests, use a **disposable simulator/browser with fictional profiles and initial Zoo state**; compare after Add as New, Replace Progress, and Replace All, including a v2 import and failed-import rollback. Do NOT test destructive Replace All on the existing production family. Neither v3 format nor structurally valid JSON alone proves complete restoration, particularly for already-missing v2 values; preserve private older backups.

## HISTORICAL — Owner follow-up: first-post-mic Flashcards instruction audio and iOS Grown-up Check keyboard (November 23, 2026)

**Baseline:** [PR #197](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/197) is **MERGED** into `ios-prep` at `3e90d97f8eef744a5248eee93a37332945561bee`. Owner reports the newly granted microphone now records successfully from the same first Flashcards Record action; the new permission icons, charcoal Skip, smaller welcome splash and shorter gate text all work well. However, the first spoken Flashcards instructions **after that newly authorized microphone recording** display a red `Instructions couldn't play. Tap Replay Instructions.` toast; manual **Replay Instructions** then plays correctly. The shared math Grown-up Check also automatically summons the iOS numeric keyboard, partially obscuring the popup before the parent hides/reopens the keyboard.

**Candidate fix** `fix/ios-flashcards-post-mic-instructions-parent-keyboard-20261123` → `ios-prep` (OPEN until owner approval): the existing production-native Flashcards recorder tracks whether the current capture began from a **fresh first-time iOS microphone authorization**. After the child explicitly taps **Stop**, only once the native Swift `finishCapture` promise confirms a retained take and restored audio session, the recorder calls existing `BibloZooNativeLearnTracks.primeFromGesture()` *before* notifying Flashcards to display its comparison screen and begin its first spoken instruction. This reuses the just-completed, child-initiated Stop action; NativeSound still owns OS activation decisions and retains all existing external-audio/interruption limits. No unsolicited auto-activation at startup, on background/foreground, during capture, on failed/empty take, after a timeout, or for subsequent already-granted microphone captures. No new mic permission/parent check; no Swift change and no Web/PWA/Audio Test behavior change. The precise first-authorization instruction failure cause is a **likely narration gesture-lease/audio-session handoff issue**, not yet proven by a captured native reason code. If this test still fails, collect a read-only error reason rather than expanding automatic activation.

**Math popup fix:** `verse_games/shared/parental-gate.js` now focuses the non-editable equation (`tabindex="-1"`) when the math challenge first appears and after a wrong answer, instead of automatically focusing the numeric text input. Parents tap **Answer** to open the keyboard; no actual math/cancel/3-attempt/security logic changes. The equation's programmatic focus has no decorative blue outline; the Answer field and buttons keep their visible focus rings. The shared native-only dialog still preserves VoiceOver announcements, keyboard Tab containment, and scrollable safe-area sizing. Native Audio Test and PWA have no shared Kids gate.

**Tests and acceptance:** `scripts/validate_kids_setup_owner_feedback.js` executes the actual native recorder with mock permission/session handoff, verifying one first mic request, successful capture, exactly one narration prime **after** Swift native successful Stop response, no duplicate prompt, no prime for denied/failed-restore/hidden/previously-granted captures. `scripts/validate_parental_gate_pr1.js` tests equation focus, no auto keyboard, manual Answer focus, wrong-answer retry, VoiceOver-safe focus and unchanged math/cancel lifecycle. CI runs both in source and the existing packaged/web checks. These tests cannot show a real Apple keyboard or prove native first-instruction audibility: **owner needs to test on disposable Not Determined simulator, iPhone/large text/VoiceOver, and manually approve merge**. Never reset installed real-family profiles; keep `main` unchanged.

**Owner-reported Flashcards first-grant bug:** When a family skipped optional permissions, then granted microphone from Flashcards' first-use parent check, the first Record attempt incorrectly opened **Continue without Recording**. Recording worked when retried. The mechanism is not proven by phone logs; suspected transient AVAudioSession/output readiness immediately after the native iOS system sheet. This patch changes only the production native JS Flashcards adapter: wait ~220ms after a *fresh* approved mic prompt, then run the existing native output barrier and capture prepare. Already-granted audio failures are not silently retried. Re-run same original 3-2-1 countdown only after preparation success. If real native audio remains unavailable, existing safe Continue without Recording fallback still applies. Do NOT claim an iPhone fix until owner reproduces/retests the first-prompt scenario.

**CI and owner acceptance:** New `node scripts/validate_kids_setup_owner_feedback.js` runs a VM of the real native recorder with simulated first permission approval and initial output or capture-preparation failures. It checks one OS request, one math gate, one countdown, no duplicate first request, a single retry, interruption, denied/already granted, CSS/wording and the two real PNG files. Included in source and packaged GitHub CI alongside existing Kids PR1–4 and setup tests. **Owner phone testing, real iOS permission alert and accessibility verification are required before merging.** Test on a disposable simulator for Not Determined, or use approved non-destructive permission-state changes; never reset the 10 production Zookeepers or 11 Audio Test profiles for this patch. ## HISTORICAL — Kids parental gate PR4 implementation handoff before #195 merge (November 23, 2026)

**Foundation:** [PR #194](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/194) is **merged** into `ios-prep` at `7a4a26666e0d8a54f4d43439577439cd09f4ca60`. The owner confirms the new `A − B + C` math problem and child-accessible **Manage/Edit Zookeeper name/picture** both **work great** on their phone; earlier PR 1–3 sharing, Settings/backups and outgoing links were also owner-tested. [PR #195](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/195), branch `feat/ios-kids-pr4-permissions-audit-20261123`, is **OPEN / unmerged and not owner-accepted**; final App Review, SDK privacy and native Xcode/device authorization tests remain OPEN. User authorizes phone testing with the existing profiles and regards them as replaceable; still avoid unintended data erasure, profiles in commits, or merging to public `main`.

**Kids PR4 focus:** Shared `verse_games/shared/kids-first-permission.js` is loaded by **both** `index.html` and standalone Ghost Writer. It checks the real native permission status at the time of the user action and calls the existing shared one-shot `BibloZooParentalGate` only for **`undetermined`**. The controller denies stale/hidden/duplicate requests or missing gate/native identity, does not store a reusable permission key, and never blocks subsequent **already granted** mic Record or Photos Save taps. Already denied access remains denied; iPhone Settings is the way to reconsider OS denial.

**Flashcards microphone:** Actual native `BibloZooMicSessionProbe.getPermissionStatus` reads `AVAudioSession.recordPermission` with no prompt; `start()` gates a fresh prompt before `requestFirstPermission`, without changing existing countdown/record/replay/voice RAM flow. Native ordinary `requestPermission` refuses to trigger the first OS prompt in production; Audio Test retains its original DEV diagnostic flow. **Ghost Writer Add Photos:** Native `getPhotosPermissionStatus` reads PhotoKit `.addOnly`; Save Picture / Save Video call Grown-up Check and `requestPhotosPermission` **before** making PNG or encoding MP4 only if first-time access is needed. Ordinary native `saveImage` and `finishVideo` refuse to trigger a first-time PhotoKit authorization via `saveToPhotos`. Subsequent saves with granted PhotoKit access proceed without new math. **Share Picture / Video remain per-share gated** by PR2 and are not conflated with Photos Save; a Photos/Files background import uses PHPicker/UIDocumentPicker without a broad permission request, so no new gate.

**Bypass and limitations:** Source inventory checks native direct save-to-Photos cannot solicit permission, native ordinary mic request cannot solicit first permission, active per-share JS gate remains at Ghost Writer handlers, no WebView download fallback on iOS share, and Settings/outgoing links retain PR3 safeguards. New explicit native `requestFirstPermission` / `requestPhotosPermission` entrypoints are callable at the Capacitor bridge after the JS Grown-up Check, so this is **not a cryptographically attested/native-only proof of a parent**. No claim of jailbreak/script-injection immunity, legal verified parental consent, or blanket Apple approval. Native UIActivityViewController direct bridge uses JS-side per-share protection; native bridge hardening vs a compromised WebView remains an audit caveat. No production Child personal data is transmitted by these permission probes.

**Validation:** `scripts/validate_parental_gate_pr4_permissions.js` executes cancellation, duplicate, stale, hidden, already-granted, denied, Audio Test isolation, exact native method/source-path checks and bundle inclusion; CI runs it in source/packaged jobs alongside earlier gate regressions and Swift frontend parser, but **Linux cannot compile/link Xcode or show a genuine iOS OS permission sheet**. For first-time testing, reset **only** Photos/Microphone permission decisions in iOS Settings or use a disposable simulator/clean iOS install after making any desired backup; never reset all content just for this test. Follow [TESTING.md](TESTING.md). Need owner approval after genuine fresh-prompt, denied/already-granted and accessibility tests before merging PR4.

## HISTORICAL — Grown-up Check easier arithmetic and child-safe Zookeeper editing (November 23, 2026)

**Baseline:** [PR #193](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/193) is **MERGED** into `ios-prep` at `d9532717742e5f4a047cb0139f35c626bb352678`. The owner explicitly reports **all PR 3 iPhone Grown-up Check tests passed** (including Settings, Ghost Writer and outgoing links). The owner is comfortable testing on the phone with existing installed profiles and says the saved data is not sensitive or irreplaceable; nevertheless no tests or PR changes should silently erase real profiles.

**Focused follow-up before PR 4:** [PR #194](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/194) (OPEN, unmerged, pending CI/owner phone retest), branch `fix/ios-kids-gate-ux-math-profile-edit-20261123` is a review candidate **not yet owner-tested or merged**. The user requested shared math questions to be **two-digit minus two-digit plus one-digit**, with final answer **never greater than 99** and preferably easier than multiplication. Implementation: randomized `A − B + C` with **A=20–99**, **B=10..A−1**, **C=1–9**. Thus B is always two-digit, subtraction is nonnegative and the result is always **2–98**. Shared controller renders `A − B + C = ?`; wrong-answer retries, three-strike denial, focus/cancel/lifecycle protection, and Ghost Writer/Settings/links all retain the same gate. Apple does not prescribe a specific arithmetic formula; this still requires real usability and compliance review for Kids ages 6–8.

**Child-facing Zookeeper personalization:** From **Settings**, **Manage [Zookeeper]** no longer needs an adult check just to navigate. Within Manage, **Edit [Zookeeper]** directly opens the existing name/picture editor; `submitProfileEditor` edits only `name` and `avatarVerseId`, with name validation intact. Manage's **Add Zookeeper, individual backup, reset, delete, erase family** and Settings **family export/import/reset/erase** still invoke the parent check (one unlock per uninterrupted parent-area visit). The editor navigation naturally invalidates prior in-memory parent authorization because `profile_editor` is not a parent-management screen; a later protected action prompts again. **Switch Zookeeper** remains child-accessible. This is a child UX change only, not a change in backup, destructive-confirmation or permission behavior.

**Test/acceptance:** Update PR 1 + PR 2 math regressions and PR 3 synthetic UI button dispatch tests. Require both GitHub Actions Node regressions and Production web build at final feature head; run `node scripts/validate_parental_gate_pr1.js`, `node scripts/validate_parental_gate_pr2_ghost_share.js`, `node scripts/validate_parental_gate_pr3_settings.js` and `npm run build`. Owner: review purple Grown-up Check from local Safari preview, then test iPhone Settings → Manage → Edit without modal, saving name/picture, then tap **Back Up Zookeeper** and confirm a math gate still appears, and cancel; check family export, external link, Ghost Writer Share each still gated, with new format and bounds. Do not use a successful test as permission to merge automatically. **PR 4 intentionally deferred until owner reports this follow-up passes**, then first-time iOS mic/Photos authorization behavior and end-to-end bypass/accessibility audit.

No PR merge, app install, real-data test, or App Store approval is claimed in this implementation note. The shared `verse_games/shared/parental-gate.css` now uses **app purple `#7f66c6`**, white popup copy, `text-wrap: balance` subtitle, borderless white buttons with purple text, and the existing `verse_images/zoobreak_lock.png` art with **no background circle** instead of an emoji. **Parent-area design:** New `verse_games/shared/parental-gate-settings.js` is loaded in main `index.html`. Native production Settings sensitive actions ask the existing math challenge, with **one ephemeral approval per continuous Settings/Zookeeper Manage/Privacy visit**, tied to active profile and foreground. A further manage task in that uninterrupted visit does not re-prompt. Navigation outside those screens, profile switching, app background, pagehide/popstate or an unknown native identity revokes access. No authorization is stored on disk. Switch Zookeeper remains a child-available navigation action and invalidates the parent visit.

**Defense in depth and limits:** Both family and individual exports re-check authorization **before** constructing JSON and at the native share/download fallback. File import from Settings is authorized before the picker and before FileReader/restore; destructive on-screen math confirmation remains a **separate** data-loss confirmation, rechecked before mutation. The dedicated `fromFirstProfile` onboarding restore flow is preserved as a separate scenario. Some platform-native handoffs/pickers may background the app and intentionally revoke access; **owner must test** and report if a fresh check is needed to resume. JS parent gating is not unforgeable native authorization; PR 4 retains native bypass/permission review and SDK/privacy/App Review acceptance. The PWA `main` and Audio Test are unchanged.

**Regression/testing:** New `scripts/validate_parental_gate_pr3_settings.js` exercises live session revocation, profile change, outgoing cancellation, fail-closed identity, backup JSON/share/download fallback and shared art/CSS packaging. Run it with prior PR 1/2 + native export tests, then `npm run build` and repeat; GitHub Actions Node regression and production web build must pass on **exact final PR head**. For native acceptance use only a disposable simulator and invented profile/JSON, not actual family data; test Settings privacy/credits text, guarded outgoing mail/link, backup exports and the separate destructive confirmation, lock on exit/profile switch/background and unchanged Ghost Writer share. [Full test guide](TESTING.md). **PR 3 remains pending owner merge and test; PR 4 first-time OS mic/Photos gate, native bypass/accessibility audit, and App Review disclosure remain pending.**

**Owner-reported PR 1 acceptance:** [PR #190](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/190) and Safari correction [PR #191](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/191) are **MERGED** into `ios-prep`, latest baseline `fdda49bc2bfdcc07792a9a190e809c73b7891704`. The owner explicitly confirmed the second local Mac tester now **passes all checks**. This is owner-reported local preview acceptance, **not** a Ghost Writer native share/device acceptance result.

**PR 2 status:** [PR #192](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/192) is **OPEN / unmerged** on feature branch `feat/ios-parental-gate-pr2-ghost-share-20261123` starts from that baseline and is scoped to **native Ghost Writer Share Picture / Share Video** in both Simple and More Options Remix, plus the owner's requested easier math range. **Open for owner review; not merged or simulator-tested** until evidence is recorded. The one shared Grown-up Check now chooses the first number from **11–29 excluding 20** (18 allowed numbers), then multiplies by 2–9 and adds 1–9; test expectations updated. Numbers ending in zero are still forbidden.

**Implementation:** Shared `verse_games/shared/ghost-writer-share-approval.js` is loaded in Ghost Writer after the common `parental-gate.js` and before game logic. Each native-production Share Picture/Share Video tap requires fresh math approval before any output canvas/PNG or native MP4 `beginVideo` is created. The adapter fails closed if native production identity or the gate is missing; it denies double-taps/concurrent attempts, cancellation and stale Remix state. The native PNG exporter rechecks a current action callback before assets/canvas/native Share; MP4 video rechecks throughout frame creation and before `finishVideo`, cancels stale temporary jobs and never silently falls back to WKWebView downloads. The native share adapter allows **Audio Test only** to keep its previous diagnostics share path; browser/PWA remains unchanged and has no native share controls. Neither Save Picture/Video to Photos nor Replay, editing, import or underlying Swift export bridge behavior is intentionally changed. **PR 3 Settings/backups/outgoing links and PR 4 first-new native permission prompts/bypass audit remain undone.** Native bridge defense in depth is a later audit requirement; do not claim final App Review/Kids compliance from JS-only gates.

**Regression and owner acceptance:** `node scripts/validate_parental_gate_pr1.js`, `node scripts/validate_parental_gate_pr2_ghost_share.js`, existing `node scripts/validate_native_ghost_writer_export.js`, then `npm run build` + new gate tests; both Node and packaged build GitHub Actions jobs must pass at exact final PR head. Owner tests on a **disposable simulator with fictional inputs**, not the 10 real production or 11 Audio Test Zookeepers. Test Share Picture and Share Video separately in Simple and More Options: cancel/three wrong answers never open a sheet or begin media export; correct math opens **only one** selected iOS share sheet; leaving/backgrounding cancels pending; Save Picture/Video remains ungated for previously granted Photos access. Full checklist in [TESTING.md](TESTING.md). **Do not reinstall/reset family apps, merge into `main`, select Kids category, or begin PR 3 before owner approval.**

### Previous PR 1 handoff, preserved for history

## HISTORICAL — PR 1 Grown-up Check Safari preview correction (November 23, 2026)

**Owner test outcome:** [PR #190](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/190) was **MERGED into `ios-prep` at `0532f4495e3c8878afe3c8b84a7687e38b3eec33`** after both exact-head CI jobs passed. The owner then tested `tests/parental-gate-preview.html` and reported: Try Grown-up Check opened the two-button intro; **Not Now closed correctly, but I'm a Grown-up also closed instead of displaying math**. This is an **owner-reported FAIL** of visual acceptance, not a full gate acceptance.

**Fix candidate:** [PR #191](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/191) (OPEN/unmerged, CI and owner retest pending) on feature branch `fix/ios-parental-gate-preview-dismissal-20261123` targets `ios-prep` and changes only the shared `verse_games/shared/parental-gate.js`, its synthetic regression, and docs. The controller originally registered an unconditional `window.blur` cancellation; Safari/WebKit can generate blur during transition into a text input/keyboard, immediately calling `finish(false)`. The correction avoids treating bare window blur as confirmed app backgrounding. It continues to cancel on actual hidden/visibilitychange, pagehide, popstate, explicit Cancel and stale/removed dialog, and requires visible native identity for approval. The intro button now passes `() => renderQuestion()` rather than passing a click Event into the feedback argument. The regression simulates WebKit input-focus blur and verifies the equation stays visible and the click Event is not rendered as text. **The suspected cause is not confirmed by a captured Safari trace; owner retest is required.** There is still no production share/settings/permission wiring. Do not install over real production or Audio Test family profiles.

**Acceptance:** Owner should merge the follow-up PR after exact-head Node/web-build CI passes, pull `ios-prep`, then reopen `tests/parental-gate-preview.html` in Safari and check Not Now, I'm a Grown-up → equation, correct/wrong answer, tab background cancellation, Escape, and display sizing. Record actual owner PASS/FAIL in next handoff. PRs 2–4 remain pending.

**PR 1 implementation boundaries:** One reusable `verse_games/shared/parental-gate.js` controller and `.css` are referenced by main `index.html` and standalone Ghost Writer `index.html`. They are active only when the installed native iOS production identity is established by the existing early `BibloZooNativeOnlyOutput` guard (`enabled:true`, `devToolsAllowed:false`) plus Capacitor iOS. The separate Audio Test identity, ordinary browser/PWA and Android are excluded. The gate is two-stage (Not Now / I'm a Grown-up, then randomized math), accessible dialog/label/live message/keyboard focus, and executes only one approved callback; no approval token/state is stored. Cancel, wrong answers, rapid second request, missing random source, window focus loss, tab background, page navigation and missing dialog deny the pending action. No production action calls the gate in PR 1 — **Ghost Writer sharing, backups, outgoing links and OS permissions are NOT yet protected**. Do not use this PR as proof of Kids Category compliance.

**Testing and preview:** `node scripts/validate_parental_gate_pr1.js` runs synthetic DOM/unit tests. An independent **unshipped** `tests/parental-gate-preview.html` visually simulates native identity for local Safari/Chrome UI review; it is excluded from `dist/` and never attached to real data. Instructions in [TESTING.md](TESTING.md). CI and owner visual acceptance are independent; do not claim Xcode or physical-device success from Node tests. No real Zookeeper data, backups, audio, photos, native Swift or PWA release was modified. Keep original production 10 and Audio Test 11 profiles safe. ### App Store release checklist — intentionally delay the $99/year membership

**Goal:** Complete every useful task that does **not** require paid Apple distribution first. Apple states that a paid Apple Developer Program membership is not required merely to install/test an app on your own device with Xcode; enrollment is required when moving into distribution services. The current US Apple Developer Program fee is **$99 USD per membership year**. Therefore, do **not** buy the membership simply because release preparation has begun. Buy it at the explicit **MEMBERSHIP GATE** below, when the remaining work actually depends on App Store Connect/TestFlight/distribution signing.

Official references:
- Apple Developer Program enrollment and current fee: https://developer.apple.com/help/account/membership/program-enrollment/
- App Review Guidelines: https://developer.apple.com/app-store/review/guidelines/
- Current SDK/upload requirements: https://developer.apple.com/news/upcoming-requirements/
- App Store Connect workflow/help: https://developer.apple.com/help/app-store-connect/

Do not automatically enable every storefront if a country/region may impose extra game, religious-content or regulatory obligations.

App Review notes should explain the child-focused Bible-memory purpose, no-login/local-data model, optional microphone recording, Photos save action, offline/bundled content, and any feature that could otherwise confuse a reviewer.

Prefer a coherent 5–7 image story using fictional Zookeepers/pets/progress.

- [ ] **Use the current Apple toolchain before paying.** Apple currently requires App Store uploads to be built with **Xcode 26 or later using the iOS 26 SDK or later**. Install/update Xcode now, build BibloZoo with that toolchain, and fix toolchain/compiler/privacy/resource issues before paying. The deployment target can remain iOS 15.0 because it is above Apple's current upload minimum.

Inspect Xcode warnings, bundle contents, icon/launch screen, privacy report and resources. A later paid-team App Store archive will still be required.

Preserve the private 10 production + 11 Audio Test profile datasets and separate Zoo rescue/surprise state; do not use them for destructive release experiments.
- [ ] **Pre-payment GO/NO-GO.** Do not enroll yet if any known blocker remains in code, legal/content rights, privacy, current-Xcode build, store copy, screenshots, seller identity, Kids strategy or support pages. At this checkpoint the project should be ready enough that the next useful steps are distribution-specific.

#### MEMBERSHIP GATE — pay here, not earlier

- [ ] **Enroll in the Apple Developer Program only now.** Use the Apple Account intended to own the app, with two-factor authentication and correct legal identity. Current US fee: **$99/year**. This is the point where the paid membership becomes materially necessary because the next work uses paid-team distribution signing, App Store Connect app records/uploads and TestFlight.
- [ ] **If enrolling as an individual, accept that the owner's personal legal name will be the App Store seller.** If an organization seller name is desired, stop and complete the legal-entity/D-U-N-S preparation before purchase rather than paying first and sorting identity later.
- [ ] **Keep auto-renewal/renewal ownership understood.** Record which Apple Account is Account Holder and where renewal/billing notices go. The goal is to spend the membership year on distribution/testing/release, not unfinished preflight work.

#### PHASE B — paid membership / App Store Connect / TestFlight

- [ ] **Accept current Apple agreements.** In the developer account/App Store Connect Business area, accept the agreements required to create/distribute a free app. Do not add paid-app/IAP contracts unless BibloZoo actually needs them.
- [ ] **Move Xcode signing to the paid team.** In Signing & Capabilities, select the paid developer team, retain the intended bundle ID `com.andydoane.biblozoo`, and let automatic signing manage the normal certificates/profiles unless there is a concrete reason not to.
- [ ] **Create/verify the App ID and App Store Connect app record.** Create the iOS app record before uploading a build. Use the existing bundle identifier; choose the final app name, primary language and an internal SKU. Treat bundle-ID changes as a serious migration, not cosmetic cleanup.
- [ ] **Set version/build numbers for the first upload.** Keep marketing version `1.0` if that is still the intended public release. Increment `CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION` for every new uploaded build (1, 2, 3, ... as needed).
- [ ] **Create a fresh paid-team Release archive with current Xcode/iOS SDK.** Do not reuse an old archive made under an earlier toolchain/team. Re-run production web build + Capacitor sync immediately before archiving. Validate the archive in Organizer.
- [ ] **Upload the build to App Store Connect.** Resolve processing, signing, entitlement, privacy-manifest, export-compliance or validation warnings before inviting testers.
- [ ] **Complete App Privacy.** Enter the privacy-policy URL and truthful data-collection answers, including third-party SDK behavior. Keep this aligned with the repo privacy policy and actual binary.
- [ ] **Complete export compliance.** Answer encryption questions consistently with the preflight classification and provide documentation only if actually required.
- [ ] **Complete age rating, content rights and regulatory declarations.** Finish the current age-rating questionnaire, content-rights questions, DSA trader status, any encryption/compliance prompts, and any region-specific declarations.
- [ ] **Set pricing/tax/availability.** For a free 1.0, choose Free and select the intended countries/regions deliberately. Add more storefronts later if desired rather than making unresolved regulatory assumptions.
- [ ] **Enter final product-page metadata.** Add name/subtitle/description/keywords/copyright, Support URL, Privacy Policy URL, screenshots and any optional promotional text/app previews.
- [ ] **Configure TestFlight.** Add beta description, feedback email and “What to Test.” Start with a small internal test group. External testers can be added later; Apple's current limits are up to 100 internal App Store Connect testers and up to 10,000 external testers, and builds are testable for up to 90 days.
- [ ] **Test the actual TestFlight-delivered binary.** This matters even after extensive Xcode testing because TestFlight exercises Apple's production distribution/signing/package path. Use disposable data first. Verify clean install, update from one TestFlight build to another, first-launch flows, permissions, offline behavior, audio, iPhone/iPad layouts, backups with test data and representative end-to-end learning/game routes.
- [ ] **Fix TestFlight defects with new build numbers.** Keep marketing version 1.0 while incrementing build number for each candidate. Repeat until no known release blocker remains.
- [ ] **Perform final metadata/privacy consistency check.** Compare the exact TestFlight candidate against App Store description, privacy labels, screenshots, permission strings and review notes. The listing must describe the binary actually being submitted.

#### PHASE C — App Review and release

- [ ] **Write reviewer-friendly App Review notes.** State clearly that BibloZoo is a Bible-verse memorization/education app with interactive games and local family profiles; no account is required; progress is local; microphone recording is optional and local to the attempt; Photos access occurs only when the user explicitly saves applicable output; there are no ads/analytics/tracking if that remains true; and packaged learning/media content is bundled. Explain unusual navigation or permission flows the reviewer should know.
- [ ] **Choose the final build and add it for review.** Confirm all required metadata/compliance fields are complete, select the exact tested build, then use App Store Connect's Add for Review → Submit for Review flow.
- [ ] **Use manual release for 1.0.** Prefer “Manually release this version” so App Review approval does not immediately publish the first release. Once status becomes Pending Developer Release, perform one last review of the listing/build decision and then press Release This Version when ready.
- [ ] **Respond to App Review directly and factually.** If rejected, preserve the review message, identify the actual guideline/question, fix or explain the narrow issue, upload a new build only when required, and document the result here. Do not redesign unrelated areas during review unless necessary.
- [ ] **After approval/release, verify the public App Store page and installation.** Confirm seller name, privacy label, screenshots, description, support/privacy links, supported devices and fresh public install behavior.
- [ ] **Tag/archive the shipped source.** Record the exact Git SHA, App Store version/build number, archive/Xcode version, submission date, review outcome and release date. Consider a Git tag such as `ios-1.0` once the owner confirms the exact shipped commit.
- [ ] **Continue membership only while distribution maintenance is desired.** Keep renewal ownership clear. Future updates should repeat privacy/content-rights/toolchain/TestFlight checks proportionate to their changes rather than treating 1.0 approval as permanent compliance.

### Immediate next release-prep action

**Next: review/merge this documentation-only acceptance-evidence PR to `ios-prep`, then finish final App Privacy/third-party SDK and remaining Release-device checks.** Public HTTPS policy and the listed scoped simulator checks are owner-confirmed; avoid re-running them without a specific remaining gap. Do not equate a blank Xcode PDF and zero observed proxy requests with full no-collection clearance. Preserve original family data and keep unresolved Chalkboard/ZapSplat rights on the pre-distribution checklist.

**Still do not enroll in the Apple Developer Program.** The checklist deliberately places the $99/year fee after all no-membership code/content/privacy/store-material work.

## HISTORICAL (premerge snapshot) — Follow-up [PR #176](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/176): responsive fifth-round pet-picture density + scroll-safe shared cards (2026-11-22 EST)

**Stable baseline and owner feedback:** [IP10 #175](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/175) **MERGED** to `ios-prep` at `0cc87ee931baf6e94bc6e7afc7c8d6ef3c3a0e87`. Owner physically tested its Verse Scramble picture hunt and Verse Launch rounded second control popup and reports **“It's working nicely”**. Treat those observations as **OWNER ACCEPTED**, without inferring unreported device/rotation matrix or independent Bounce “1 ball left!” proof (**NOT TESTED**). IP10 is working in owner's observed paths; extra space in fifth-round image playfield and scroll-induced card pop are new follow-ups.

**PR #176:** `feat/ip11-picture-density-scroll-aware-cards` → `ios-prep`, **OPEN / UNMERGED; OWNER DEVICE PENDING**. Fifth-round Scramble no longer selects 12/16/20 photos by Easy/Medium/Hard. It seeds a 125-item catalog of **existing public bundled** pet art using only public verse ID, then uses an invisible on-board probe of actual CSS field/tile dimensions to fill ~90% of safely non-overlapping grid cells, identically across modes for the same device/viewport. Jittered/shuffled placements use the larger iPad and smaller iPhone playfields proportionally; exactly one always-visible target, distinct decoys, bounded image prewarming (up to 28 + target), unframed `object-fit:contain` picture buttons, keyboard and pointer support. Mid-round orientation shrink may hide extra decoys but cannot hide the target. Rounds 1–4 remain **110 colored letters** on every mode, with prior speed, timing, scores, rewards and menus untouched.

**Card gesture follow-up:** The original shared `.practice-game-card:active`/`.new-verse-card:active` CSS and app-wide `touchstart`/`pointerdown` pop produced false visual/audio presses when scrolling across cards. A scoped shared `app.js` touch tracker now monitors distance, scroll offset and pointer cancellation on scrollable practice/game/Playground/Learn/Read/verse cards, and only sends existing Web/native UI sound + pressed animation on confirmed stationary release. No `preventDefault`/touch-scroll blocking, no change to card `onclick` navigation, mouse and keyboard paths remain; native AVFoundation audio is **not** rewritten. CSS prevents the browser's premature `:active` depression on coarse pointers. Owner must test real iOS touch/Swift UI pop, single pop after tap, and normal scroll momentum.

**Verse Launch copy:** Post-flight travel cutscene retains only “Reach the moon!” and “Dodge the asteroids.”, removing pre-chooser legacy arrows/keys/tilt steering text. Separate existing Tilt/Drag chooser/popup, timing and sound are unchanged.

**Validation and safety:** Workflow continues full older IP01–IP10 and native/privacy/Swift/web build, updates IP10 public art count checks, and adds `scripts/validate_ip11_picture_density_scroll_cards.js` executing real production density/placement and synthetic pointer/touch native sound/scroll interactions, unchanged original letter/target/winner contracts and cutscene copy. **PR CI and physical iPad/iPhone testing pending**. Do not claim Linux checks provide Xcode SDK build, WebKit hardware, AVAudioSession output or actual visual acceptance. Preserve original **10 production and separate 11 Audio Test** private Zookeepers, backups/v2, independent Zoo rescue/surprise storage and releases; do not reset, reinstall or touch `main`. Owner controls merge.

**Owner WHAT TO TEST** (record PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED with device/orientation): (1) Scramble **fifth round** on iPad landscape/portrait and iPhone: field looks as comfortably full as letters but raw pet art is clear, no overlaps/crops/masks, same count for Easy/Medium/Hard at same geometry, exact preview/target and pointer/child win; rotate before/after fifth begins. (2) rounds 1–4 still 110 colored letters; clock/wrong-photo penalty, mode pointer speed, one-time score/medals and Welcome Back unchanged. (3) Scroll External Games, Playground, Learn/Listen/Read and New Verse/My Verses by starting finger on cards: no depress or pop, normal momentum; stationary tap makes one pop/normal navigation, test native iOS, optionally desktop mouse and keyboard. (4) Verse Launch travel text has two lines only then old chooser and Drag/Tilt work; no arrows. Report any real-device audio or layout defect before merging.

## HISTORICAL — IP09 premerge corrective snapshot, [PR #174](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/174) (2026-11-22 EST; superseded by #175)

**Live verified base:** The owner **merged IP09 [#173](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/173)** into stable `ios-prep` at **`680e8e0381f6c367ab7cac647d7d84b148a3b993`** on November 22, 2026. Its exact post-merge [GitHub Actions #45071646637](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/45071646637) completed **SUCCESS**, including the Node regression and production web jobs. The earlier #173 final PR head `10750eb52c2f335b2a39f0a750749a7d484276bd` also had [green Actions #45071373743](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/45071373743). **Owner tested merged IP09 and reported two Verse Launch issues; this is NOT an IP09 hardware PASS or a per-device matrix:** (1) previously absent on-screen left/right arrows reappeared **when selecting Drag**, obstructing finger steering; (2) main verse-building wrong-answer red flash stops above the bottom game padding, leaving an unflashed strip. No broader conclusions about Bounce's iPadOS magnifier, physical Tilt permissions, orientation or held App Switcher were supplied. Separate randomized Feeding Bounce **“1 ball left!”** physical observation remains **NOT TESTED**.

**Focused corrective [PR #174](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/174):** Feature branch `fix/ip09-verse-launch-arrows-and-flash` from the verified merged `ios-prep` SHA, target **`ios-prep`**, **OPEN / UNMERGED / OWNER DEVICE PENDING**. At phone widths the previous page-1 equalization is **untouched**, and on iPad/phone pages 2–3 still match the taller transitions. No fixed viewport clipping/forced scroll-disable.
2. **Onboarding page-2 numbered list unbalanced.** In `styles.css`, only the page-2 ordered list `ol,li` sets `text-wrap:wrap` so the list's three items have natural line breaks. The existing balanced `.todo-tutorial-body` paragraphs elsewhere remain unchanged.

Normal "Keep Practicing" / Game Mix "Continue Mix" actions remain unmodified; the Daily To-Do "Back to Mission" variant keeps its care message below that button. Constrain care text max-width to reveal area, preserve animation and `aria-live`, focus and navigation.
4. **Daily Zoo To-Do full profile picture.** In `daily_zoo_todo.css` **only** inside `.todo-dev-screen.is-daily-mode`, override circular/profile-cover defaults with transparent, uncropped `.daily-zoo-todo-profile-picture` wrapper (`border-radius:0; overflow:visible`) and child `.profile-picture-img {object-fit:contain}`. **Stable owner-merged baseline, verified before editing:** `ios-prep` SHA `97cf88a403a870ecab668864d0778849ba9ffcf0`; owner merged [IP01 PR #160](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/160) at 2026-11-22T00:44:57Z, after final PR-head Node/Web CI PASS. [Post-merge stable Actions #44968131740](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44968131740) **SUCCESS**. Owner says they are **testing IP01 now and will report results later**: **IP01 merged; OWNER iPad/iPhone TEST RESULTS PENDING**, not an owner PASS/COMPLETE claim. Previous IP01 "CURRENT / open / CI pending" passage directly below is **HISTORICAL**. Earlier WHEEL-01 #158 was merged and owner iPad accepted; R18 #157 accepted. No preexisting open PRs at new IP02 start.

**Owner request:** Implement grouped [IP02 (2 findings)](IPAD-PLAYTEST-GROUPED-PR-ROADMAP.md) in the same stable `ios-prep` workflow. Candidate `fix/ip02-daily-next-ready-todo-mode-lock` → `ios-prep` from verified SHA `97cf88a4`. **[IP02 PR #161](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/161) OPEN / NOT MERGED; real iPad and iPhone NOT TESTED.** Owner explicitly prioritizes Daily Questions Next responsiveness and fixed Daily To-Do game mode; preserve iPhone layout, audio recovery, rewards, scoring and normal mode selection outside Daily To-Do.

**IP02-1 — Daily Questions Next source explanation and candidate repair:** In `daily_questions.js`, clicking an answer immediately re-renders with an active Next button, while a correct answer's star-reward timer expires 900 ms later and **re-renders the entire question slide**. That mid-touch DOM replacement may discard a WebKit click. The candidate records per-question `session.nextReady`, draws **Next disabled/faded** until feedback finishes (**900 ms** with star popup, **320 ms** without), and on expiration **enables the current button in-place** rather than re-rendering away a child's touch target. Expiring feedback removes the transient star element in-place. Existing one-answer star-credit logic is preserved. A valid tap after readiness passes the existing single-transition guard; early/duplicate taps cannot advance. Pause for real App Switcher/Welcome Back cancels the timer and restores readiness for an already-answered question on explicit return (no auto progression). This is a **code-derived diagnosis / proposed fix**, not a verified device cause or owner acceptance.

**IP02-2 — Daily To-Do assigned mode:** `verse_games/shared/game-shell.js` Game Menu previously hid Mode Select only on Game Mix, and exposed it for Daily To-Do games despite `daily-todo-shell.js` auto-selecting an assigned mode. In the candidate, the shared Game Menu calls the **existing** `window.VerseGameBridge.isDailyTodoLaunch()` complete-context validator and hides Mode Select during a genuine Daily activity launch for all **12 Practice Games + 5 Playground activities** where the shared game menu applies. A second check in the shared Mode Select click handler blocks an unwanted restart even if a stale element survived. **Ordinary direct launches, legacy Zoo To-Do context, and a malformed/incomplete Daily token still retain the original Mode Select; Game Mix suppression and explicit per-game hide remain independent.** No mode assignment, progression, audio or return/credit routing changes.

**Tests, workflow and status:** New `scripts/validate_ip02_daily_next_mode.js` executes real Daily Questions answer/scheduler/Next/transition callbacks with fake timers/DOM, verifying one star for rapid duplicate answer taps, Next visually disabled until ready, in-place enabling with no timer-driven render, exactly-one next-page advance and wrong-answer feedback. It executes real bridge Daily To-Do validation and shared game-menu markup/click code for all 17 activities, normal/Game Mix/legacy and incomplete contexts. Existing full Node regressions, native audio and iOS resource/privacy checks, production web build and Swift source grammar CI remain in `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`. **New exact PR-head CI: PENDING at this authoring point; Apple SDK/Xcode compile/archive: NOT TESTED; physical iPad/iPhone: NOT TESTED.** Don't treat Linux CI as a real device PASS or invent the owner's unfinished IP01 test results.

**Owner acceptance after owner review and merge:** On the iPad and iPhone in both orientations, run Daily Questions with correct answers that earn first/second star, a third correct answer after cap, and wrong answers: Next initially faded/disabled until feedback completes, then responds on the **first enabled tap**, with no ignored input or double advance/reward. In Daily Questions To-Do, last Next returns/credits once; in ordinary flow last Next enters Feeding once without accidental tilt prompt/audio duplication. Pause or hold App Switcher during feedback, use actual Welcome Back, confirm Next does not get stuck and requires an explicit tap. Launch Tower of Bible, Bible Bugs and representative other games/Playground from an actual Daily To-Do task: **Mode Select absent** in Menu and task's assigned difficulty/return route preserved; direct game launch still offers Mode Select and Game Mix keeps its own locked mode. Verify Help, Mute, Close, Exit, unchanged phone layout, Web/PWA/Android smoke path. Report observed configurations and **PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED** separately; no auto-merge or device installation from this branch.

**Privacy/release boundaries:** Original 10 production Zookeepers and distinct 11 Audio Test profiles remain privately backed up and separate; family-v2 excludes Zoo rescue/surprise counters. No profile reset/import/uninstall, WebAudio fallback on native iOS, AVAudioSession modification, Xcode-signing switch, `main` merge, TestFlight, or App Store delivery. This PR updates the exact canonical `docs/PROJECT-HANDOFF.md` and the [iPad roadmap](IPAD-PLAYTEST-GROUPED-PR-ROADMAP.md), [testing guide](TESTING.md); next PR must update them again after owner IP01/IP02 results are reported.

## HISTORICAL — WHEEL-01 candidate before #158 merge and owner iPad PASS (superseded)

**Verified stable baseline:** `ios-prep` HEAD `529264938e01c03e4554f1f3bf2e32191496e4c4` after the owner's merge of [R18 PR #157](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/157) (kid-friendly How to Play for 17 activities and removed child-facing welcome version pill). [Exact post-merge stable Actions #44914877226](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44914877226) **SUCCESS**. Owner reports **R18 tested and worked; mark R18 OWNER PASS / COMPLETE** for the observed behavior. Do not imply an exhaustive 17-activity/iOS-version or device matrix from that statement. R17 promotion/closeout #155/#156 remain completed. New ordinary work branches from and targets stable `ios-prep`, never deleted integration or `main`.

**New iPad reproduction / WHEEL-01:** Owner reports that while Wheel of Bible's raining Bible Bux animation is running, Game Menu correctly pauses it; after tapping Mute and closing Menu, Bible Bux remains frozen and the menu button becomes unresponsive. Other app/UI lifecycle still operates: task-switching triggers an actionable Welcome Back. Owner reproduced it more than once; without Mute, some menu closes resumed normally. Captured Xcode log contains normal native audio preloads/play and lifecycle signals, but no pinpointed JavaScript stack trace or menu-close event; do not claim it independently proves a native AVAudioSession failure.

**Source-verified cause and scoped candidate:** In `verse_playground/wheel_of_bible/game.js`, Game Menu `onOpen` sets `nativeWheelMenuOpen=true` and pauses `welcomeClock`, queued game timers and visual animations. `onClose` invokes `primeWheelNativeFromGesture("resume")`. Its **muted** fast path incorrectly called `resumeWheelAfterReturn()` only when `!nativeWheelMenuOpen`; that flag was still true from the menu opening. This traps `welcomePaused` and the menu-open lock, blocking both the animation and future menu taps. The unmuted async-success path correctly clears the flag first, explaining the intermittent/no-mute success. Candidate `fix/ipad-wheel-menu-mute-resume` clears the menu lock then resumes the original game timers and animations on muted Close **without activating native audio**. No new game rule, audio session category, score/reward, recording, profile, bundle ID or Web/PWA/Android behavior is intended. Keep normal unmuted preflight and true OS-interruption/Welcome Back gating unchanged.

**Verification and owner gate:** New `scripts/validate_wheel_menu_mute_resume.js` executes the real production menu callback, native-preflight, freeze and thaw functions in a synthetic iPad-style reproduction: menu open → mute → Close → animation/timer resume → menu open again → unmute → Close → await successful native preflight → responsive menu. Wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`. **Automated CI / exact PR-head result: PENDING at handoff authoring; real iPad retest: NOT TESTED for this candidate.** No Xcode SDK compile or physical evidence is implied by Node assertions.

**Next action:** Owner reviews this focused PR after exact-head Node regression and production web build checks are green; owner decides whether to merge into `ios-prep`. Then pull/build/sync on the Mac and test the exact reported rainy Bible Bux moment: Menu pauses; tap Mute; Close resumes *silently*; menu can reopen; unmute; Close resumes after sound preflight; retry open/close at spin/verse/prize and check genuine App Switcher Welcome Back still works. Record PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED and any new regression in the next PR's **same canonical** `docs/PROJECT-HANDOFF.md`. Preserve private off-phone backups of 10 production and separate 11 Audio Test profiles; family-v2 omits standalone Zoo rescue/surprise stats. ## HISTORICAL R16b.2 implementation before owner Silent ON test (merged #152; superseded by R16b.3)

**Owner design approved for implementation, NOT yet iPhone-tested:** Verse Crawl starts with its original music and spoken verse; a top-right circular `#ffc751` button uses the owner's newly uploaded `verse_images/game_ui/ui_icon_music_notes.png`. Tap it once to fade the button and choose **music OFF / spoken verse only**. Tap again to restore **music ON / original music + narration**. No startup popup, hardware Silent-switch detection, global mute change, or saved preference. Each session starts in original music mode.

**Native safety boundary:** Music ON preserves the original `.soloAmbient` soundtrack/verse overlap (both respond to iPhone Silent Mode). Music OFF stops both Crawl theme and ending, awaits stop acknowledgements, and only then starts source-validated verse speech under a short-lived `.playback` essential-voice lease, so verse narration can be heard with Silent ON while optional music is never carried into the lease. Switching during a verse retires only its current player and restarts the same verse clip from its last known position; reversing the toggle stops that lease before restoring the original music. The existing interruption, external-media priority, no-autoplay, and explicit Welcome Back rules remain; no WebAudio fallback or new permissions.

**Verification gate:** New `scripts/validate_r16b2_crawl_music_toggle.js` (source contracts plus actual native JS adapter and toggle VM) is wired into GitHub Actions with prior R16a/R16b/Read/Swift-parse/web-build regressions. **Physical-iPhone testing is NOT YET DONE.** The owner must try both button directions during narration with speaker Silent ON/OFF, recovery and Exit, and make sure no false Welcome Back, audible leaked music, truncated progress or false reward occurs. CI alone cannot establish physical audio correctness. [Scoped device checklist](TESTING.md) and [focused decision history](R16B2-VERSE-CRAWL-SILENT-MODE-DECISION.md).

**PR status:** [R16b.2 PR #152](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/152) is OPEN (initially draft), **not merged**. Automated checks must pass on the final exact PR head; owner iPhone result is still pending.

Leave `ios-prep`, `main`, and the 10 original production / 11 separate Audio Test profiles intact. ## HISTORICAL R16b.1 proposal and pre-fix owner report / AUDIO-01 — owner Typewriter regression, targeted fix under review (2026-11-18)

**Merged baseline:** [R16b PR #149](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/149) merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` as `60f187a57626710e98ae7c325b5376d1160190de`; [post-merge CI 44387158750](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44387158750) **PASS**. Owner device test of that **merged** version reveals a specific new failure: **Typewriter Silent ON** key clicks quiet, but an unwanted **Welcome Back** appears before each verse segment; voice plays after the child's Let's go tap. **Typewriter Silent OFF** ordinary key clicks audible but the *same false Welcome Back* before each spoken segment. Mark Typewriter **FAIL in both positions**; do not call R16b passed. **Balloons/Fish Silent ON** owner reports as expected (**PASS for observed flow only**). **Verse Crawl Silent OFF** normal music+spoken verse (**PASS**); **Crawl Silent ON** text only, music and voice absent (**known unimplemented Option B spoken-voice gap**). No other Read activity, interruption, headphone route, or explicit OS version test is inferred.

**Source-backed diagnosis:** Typewriter's final typing alert intentionally overlaps the start of spoken narration (`playChunkAlertSound(); finishCurrentChunk()`; `stopKeySoundVoices({keepChunkAlert:true})`). R16b #149 made essential `read-narration` refuse `.playback` if *any* native Read audio clip was still running; its `voice-overlap-unsafe` refusal triggers the existing Read Welcome Back safety path even in Silent OFF. This exact mechanism is strongly supported by source but **no Xcode reason trace was supplied for this run**, so require post-fix physical iPhone verification.

**R16b.1 proposed repair:** Branch `fix/r16b1-read-typewriter-optional-key-handoff` preempts **only** fixed-source short Typewriter/Keyboard key and final-alert clips, after spoken verse has prepared and **before** its brief `.playback` lease. It never preempts Crawl music/ending, Zoo cue, other verse clips, unknown audio or scheduled Read notes; all such overlap still fails closed. Optional clips emit truthful `interrupted` (not a fake `ended` or OS lifecycle notification), so source owners can replay future keys. Global UI effects remain silent on physical Silent-ON speaker. Short end-of-chunk alert may be truncated when the verse starts. All original foreground/OS interruptions, profile/recording privacy and no background/audio auto-resume stay intact.

**Next gate:** Focused `scripts/validate_r16b1_typewriter_effect_voice_handoff.js`, adjusted R16a/R16b tests, all native/Read regressions, Swift syntax and web build must **PASS for exact proposed head**. After owner review/merge, repeat Typewriter ON and OFF to verify no false popup and real speech; then one genuine lock/App Switcher interruption must **still** cause Welcome Back. Quick Balloons/Fish and Silent-OFF Crawl checks. **R16b Crawl voice-under-Silent is NOT solved, R17 remains blocked.** Full [R16b.1 diagnosis and owner test plan](R16B1-TYPEWRITER-VOICE-HANDOFF.md). No auto-merge; no `ios-prep`/`main` or family-profile changes.

**Handoff priority:** Review owner-scoped R16b.1 PR only after exact SHA CI. Owner already demonstrated the issue in Typewriter Silent ON/OFF, no need to ask again on unchanged build. Do not mark earlier #149 broad Read acceptance PASS; #149 is **partial** with a real Typewriter defect. Waiting for a new patch to be merged, then the focused owner test. No additional bundle installs or private data sharing beyond normal protected build.


**R16b automation on initial PR head:** **PASS** at `d8f137b9d7fa1e49ee37caa700e959a6042e93bb` — [GitHub Actions run 44386375595](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44386375595), including new Read voice source/adapter VM tests, existing regressions, Swift source parsing and production web build. ## HISTORICAL R16a.2 proposal before merge/device testing / AUDIO-01 — Learn Chunks/Echo Menu false Welcome Back fix proposed (2026-11-18)

**Owner iPhone evidence and separate issues:** On physical iPhone with Silent ON and **speaker** output, owner reports **narration audible / Home UI pops and Verse Scramble effects quiet — PASS for tested Option B acoustic distinction**. With **AirPods**, all those native optional effects remain audible although debug logs show `AVAudioSessionCategorySoloAmbient` with `BluetoothA2DPOutput`. This is **route-dependent behavior** observed on the device, NOT a stale voice `.playback` lease; do **not** force special Bluetooth muting or claim native Silent-switch detection. Apple’s audio guidance emphasizes the user's choice of output/headphones and silencing nonessential audio; use normal OS category behavior rather than speculative workaround. Flashcards including recorded-voice playback still works in Silent Mode, as owner confirmed. [R16a.1 diagnostic PR #147](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/147) is merged.

**New owner regression:** During **Learn the Verse / Chunks and Echo**, tapping the **Menu** button shows the **Welcome Back** overlay instead of the Learn Menu. The accompanying Xcode transcript shows `BibloZooNativeSound.play` returning `category-not-soloambient` *during* a legitimate spoken `learn-part-...` whose essential-voice lease is temporarily `.playback`; the native effect rejection calls `forgetPlayback()`, emits `biblozooNarration type=invalidated reason=interrupted`, and triggers the JS Learn interruption/Welcome Back path. This is a **false interruption caused by an optional global UI pop**, not proof that the child left UIKit/OS active state.

**R16a.2 proposed patch:** A native sound effect request under the **same currently playing, owner-proven narration `.playback` lease** is still **rejected** (`accepted:false`, `category-not-soloambient`), but it must **not** call `forgetPlayback()` or emit a fake `interrupted` notification. All OTHER blocked cases still use the original fail-closed teardown: genuine UIKit inactive/background, pending OS interruption, media reset, external audio, or a non-owner category mismatch. Swift `BibloZooEssentialVoiceSession.isActive(owner:)` is read-only; no category/mode/volume/microphone change. The real app's existing `openLearnMenu` then owns checkpoint/pause and close-from-gesture resume; no Web fallback, autoplay, reward or profile change.

**Release gate:** R16a.2 remains **NOT DEVICE TESTED** until owner presses Menu during active **Chunks** and **Echo** narration (Silent ON, phone speaker and, if convenient, AirPods) and observes Learn Menu rather than Welcome Back, closes to resume at correct checkpoint, and then holds App Switcher/locks phone to verify genuine OS interruptions STILL use Welcome Back. Also observe unchanged silent-speaker optional effects, Flashcards recording/voice, music/AirPods and rewards; do not treat static Swift source checks as physical proof. Keep R16 overall open pending separate Read My Verse narrator/soundtrack review. No automatic merge, stable `ios-prep` and `main` untouched; no original production-device uninstall, backups or child audio uploads.

**Handoff next action:** Review focused R16a.2 PR (native sound + session lease + static regression only). After CI and owner-controlled merge, test Menu **during** Chunks/Echo spoken parts, then Close and real iOS inactive/Welcome Back. Do not re-run full Verse Scramble/AirPods matrix; speaker Silent ON behavior is already owner-PASS for those cases. No more speculative category changes before checking the true Menu interaction.

The original 10-profile production and separate 11-profile Audio Test datasets stay private and distinct.


> **Orientation snapshot, not source of truth.** Verify both `ios-prep` (stable integration) and `experiment/native-ios-audio` (isolated iOS audio research) against their actual source, current PRs, tests, and the owner's latest device evidence before acting. This document is a living guide, not proof of a merged PR or a successful iPhone test.
