## CURRENT R14 / PRACTICE-01 — Traffic Tap bonus-truck pacing ([PR #144](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/144))

**R13 / PLAY-02 OWNER PHYSICAL IPHONE PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #143](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/143) merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `113b320733604bede0b1c91f4e0dc77bcbfb268d`. The owner reports **“I merged and tested that PR and it's working great”** and explicitly authorizes marking the Ghost Writer mode-selection popup issue complete. R01–R12b were already accepted, so **R01–R13 now have owner-reported physical-iPhone PASS** for their targeted fixes, not a blanket optional route/iOS-version/device-matrix claim. The installed exact SHA, iOS version and other routes are not supplied. Historical R13 `NOT TESTED` text beneath the *historical R13* heading is pre-acceptance only.

**Owner R14 finding, `PRACTICE-01`:** Traffic Tap's bonus-truck entrance and exit travel **too slowly** as the long front/trailer crosses the field. Source diagnosis: both `startBonusTruckIntro` and `startBonusTruckOutro` in `verse_games/traffic_tap/game.js` used `Math.round(clamp(130 * trafficSpeedMultiplier(), 120, 190))` px/s, where `trafficSpeedMultiplier` scales from Easy (1.0) through Medium (1.3) to Hard (1.45) late in the verse. A ~390px synthetic phone field plus ~520px truck can require ~978px of right-to-left travel; Easy/Normal thus took **~7.5s by source math** (not a measured physical device transit). This truck also ignored the existing `VerseGameShell.getGameSpeedMultiplier()` Normal 1×, Fast 1.25× and Max 1.5× speed presets, unlike ordinary traffic.

**Focused [R14 PR #144](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/144), `fix/r14-traffic-tap-bonus-truck-pacing` → integration only:** Dedicated pure `bonusTruckTravelSpeed()` uses `Math.round(clamp(300 * trafficSpeedMultiplier() * VerseGameShell.getGameSpeedMultiplier(), 280, 560))` px/s, identical for intro and outro. Easy/Normal becomes **300 px/s**, Fast **375** and Max **450**, at least ~2.3× the previous Easy speed; higher medium/hard values safely cap at 560. Captured once at the start of each phase; no mid-crossing speed shifts. The original start `fieldWidth + 28`, right-half lane-clear requirement, per-frame `x -= speed * (dt / 1000)`, **34ms active-frame clamp**, `x < -(bonusTruckWidth + 40)` full-trailer exit gates and all original transitions stay untouched. Intro still starts the actual timed/scored bonus round **only after full exit**; outro shows results **only after full exit**. A 560px/s cap plus 34ms dt means no more than ~19px of movement per frame. **Ordinary vehicles**, streak/traffic acceleration, collisions, bonus target/score, rainbow bus, round timing, sprites, truck intro/outro music/volume, muting, audio files and native session stay unchanged. This is a visual timing/feel change only; phone/tablet rendering and readability still need owner acceptance.

**Regression and real-device gate:** New `scripts/validate_r14_traffic_tap_bonus_truck_pacing.js`, wired to `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`, executes **the real production Traffic Tap speed function, truck intro/outro, main/bonus phase branches, activity-time loop and pause** with synthetic geometry. Nine combinations of Easy/Medium/Hard × Normal/Fast/Max, correct scale/caps, original right-lane clearance, one original intro/outro cue, exact offscreen gates before reward/start/end, menu/Welcome Back pause freeze and no resume teleport, narrower phone/wider tablet. Existing N6l native Traffic Tap PCM/UI/PiP, R01–R13 regressions, Swift parser and production Web build continue. **R14 owner physical iPhone: NOT TESTED.** CI synthetic geometry is not measured iPhone animation smoothness or perceived pacing. On actual phone: play Easy/Normal to bonus transition and exit, then Fast/Max; truck should cross **noticeably faster but readable**, never clip/skip its full trailer, duplicate score/reward, change ordinary vehicle speeds, skip Bonus or prematurely render results. Test Medium/Hard if desired; game speed picker prior to bonus, Menu/held App Switcher/Apple Music/AirPods freezing and genuine Welcome Back, original sounds, target visibility, countdown/rainbow bus, pause/resume and ordinary gameplay. Report whether 300px/s looks right or needs a small R14b feel adjustment. No tests or claims made on owner's actual device by automation.

**Safety/next:** Original production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` holds **10 private irreplaceable Zookeeper profiles**; separate Audio Test `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest` has **11 DIFFERENT profiles** and backups are not interchangeable. No uninstall/reset/restore/import, child audio/PCM/profile/backup upload, or production-ID install without separate owner approval and correct verified off-device production backup. PR remains open for owner review/merge and physical test, stable `ios-prep` and `main` untouched. **R15 `AUDIO-01` Silent Mode is an owner product-policy decision PR, NOT automatic implementation or an unapproved `.soloAmbient` category change**; consider it only after R14 owner acceptance.

## HISTORICAL R13 / PLAY-02 — Ghost Writer mode-selection transient popup ([PR #143](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/143))

**R12b / READ-01 owner physical iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #142](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/142) merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `b4a85d1a213d7d4c4e501d700bf07b607b74c7c3`. The owner subsequently says **“I tested that PR and it worked.”** This resolves the verse-dependent **three-line vs two-line Typewriter intro** from the first R12 #141 iPhone test, which the owner had called **“99% working”** before R12b. **Close R12/READ-01** as owner-reported accepted with targeted physical iPhone follow-up; retain R12 original quote/source tests, Typewriter/Keyboard intro regression and all R01–R11 passes. Exact installed source SHA, iOS version and unreported optional hardware/VoiceOver routes not provided. Any earlier R12b **“NOT TESTED”** statements below the *historical* heading are pre-acceptance notes.

**R13 original owner finding `PLAY-02`:** Ghost Writer briefly flashes an unexpected popup/screen immediately after selecting a mode, then proceeds normally. The owner did **not** supply a frame capture or the exact text of that popup. We must not pretend its visual identity has been verified. The source contains a highly specific likely mechanism on native iOS: `renderModeSelect` calls `primeNativeGhostFromGesture(() => startRun(mode), "start")`; this unconditionally appended a **full-screen `#ghostNativePreparing`** panel (“Getting spooky sounds ready…”) while seven native PCM files were prepared and removed it as soon as the Promise resolved. Fast resolution can visibly paint a transient status for a frame or two. Neither the actual training “Draw each letter nice and big!” message nor Photos permission/export dialogs are evidence of a defect, and they must not be removed. The brief popup's precise identity still requires owner-device confirmation.

**Focused [R13 PR #143](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/143), branch `fix/r13-ghost-writer-mode-transition-preparing-flash` → integration only:** Delay **only the visible `#ghostNativePreparing` layer by 350ms**, using a single timer fenced by the existing native preflight generation, current pending state and `document.hidden`. Start **native `prepareFromGesture(uiTap)` synchronously on the original child mode tap** exactly as before; successful fast bank prep transitions straight to the real Training screen without a useless loading popup. If work takes longer, retain the original accessible “Getting spooky sounds ready…” overlay and Back to Playground; a denied prepare still shows the existing **Sound isn't ready / Try Again** modal with an explicit new gesture, and temporary Apple Music/PiP interruptions still use Welcome Back. Cancel the pending timer and remove temporary loading on success, actual failure, fresh retry, loading Back and native UIKit inactive even when WebKit remains visible. While the loading overlay is deferred the mode-select Back remains accessible: tapping it explicitly increments the attempt generation, clears pending action/dialog and calls native stop so **a late Promise can never unexpectedly open Training**. Duplicate mode taps are still blocked. The action/handwriting, original seven PCM bank, `.soloAmbient`, spoken effects, muted/Web/PWA output policy, external app takeover, gesture-only ownership, native route and app lifecycle are unchanged. No changes to shared shell/Swift, Ghost Writer training 1.5-second instruction, drawing, Remix, Photos/Files picker, export/permission UI, other Playground games or stored profiles.

**Automation and owner gate:** New `scripts/validate_r13_ghost_writer_mode_preparing_flash.js` in CI executes the **actual production Ghost Writer mode-select + native dialog/preflight code** against a controlled fake DOM, Promise and timer: quick success never inserts loading; truly slow work does; pending double-tap does not double-start; denial retains real Try Again; temporary external audio yields Welcome Back; Back before/after loading fences stale success; native inactivity cancels even while WebKit remains visible; warm native PCM still activates from real gesture. Existing N6r/R01–R12b, Swift parse, native-only audio isolation, export/picker and production Web build are preserved. **R13 physical iPhone: NOT TESTED.** This source candidate does not confirm the unidentified owner flash until actual visual testing. On iPhone select **Beginner and Advanced** repeatedly from Ghost Writer Mode Select; the quick success must go directly to ordinary Training with **no flickering full-screen progress**. Slow native preparation may still legitimately show a stable progress panel; real denial/Retry/Apple Music/AirPods/PiP remains safe and must not be suppressed. Check mode Back during pending, 10–15s held App Switcher, normal Training's intentional “Draw each letter nice and big!”, drawing/Next, Photo picker/export and other games. If flash persists, seek a **short non-private** video/frame description; diagnose the precise overlay rather than broad CSS hiding. Don't claim real-device PASS from CI. No auto-merge or stable `ios-prep`/`main` promotion.

**Privacy/deployment gate:** Production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` retains **10 irreplaceable private Zookeeper profiles**; separate `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest` contains **11 different profiles**. Never conflate their off-device backups, uninstall/reset/restore/import or upload child's voice/PCM/profile/backup. Production-ID app update needs separately explicit owner approval and verified correct production backup. R14 `PRACTICE-01` Traffic Tap bonus-truck pacing follows **only after R13 owner acceptance**.

## HISTORICAL R12b / READ-01 follow-up — fixed two-line Read typing intro ([PR #142](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/142))

**Owner post-merge physical-iPhone R12 finding — NEAR-PASS / FOLLOW-UP NEEDED, NOT FULLY CLOSED:** [R12 PR #141](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/141) merged to `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `686ceba9825eae342c416df113f3a30c7920f4d4`. After testing R12 on a real iPhone, owner reports it **“99% working”**: original quotation-mark grouping appears to have worked well overall, but the **Typewriter launch instruction varies its line breaks by selected verse**. For **Acts 5:29**, they saw **three lines: “Tap to” / “type” / “the verse.”**; for **Acts 16:31**, **two lines: “Tap to type” / “the verse.”** They request **consistently two lines**, sized to fill the available horizontal width and independent of the verse. This is new owner-reported device evidence, **not** R12 full PASS or proof of comprehensive R12 mode coverage. R01–R11 remain separately owner-reported physical iPhone PASS.

**Source diagnosis:** In `read_my_verse.js`, `restoreReadTypingFit` and `fitReadTypingVerseText` set `--typewriter-intro-font-size` from the **verse's own** computed/cached `fontSize`. Long verses shrink the intro, short verses enlarge it. The old intro markup only kept “the verse.” together with a `nowrap` tail; it never guaranteed the first phrase “Tap to type” remains one line. This is a deterministic source-level explanation of the observed 3-line versus 2-line wrapping, not a native/iPhone audio problem.

**Focused R12b [PR #142](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/142), branch `fix/r12b-read-typing-intro-two-line-independent-fit` → integration ONLY:** Replace the dynamic-wrap intro with exactly **two nonbreaking lines** using the original characters `Tap to type ` and `the verse.` in `renderTypingIntroText`. Keep the hidden full-sentence measuring guide + hidden yet-untyped letters, overlayed real typed characters, 75ms/letter sound/animation, 3s hold, 300ms fade, accessibility, reduced-motion rules, and first-tap gating. Remove **both verse-fit-to-intro size assignments**, leaving the existing per-verse font sizing, cache, chunk display, R12 quote grouping and source/audio indices untouched. New `fitReadTypingIntro(wrap)` measures the first and second intro guide lines in the **current screen's intro box** using the actual loaded font, fits to approximately 98% of available horizontal width while respecting two-line height, and writes only the intro-specific font variable. Runs on initial/delayed layout, font readiness and rotation. Keyboard shares the same fixed-layout geometry and retains its green font treatment; it is tested independently rather than assumed from Typewriter. Responsive fallback before first measurement; no content/verse ID input to the intro fitter.

**Regression and physical gate:** `scripts/validate_r12b_read_typing_intro_independent_fit.js` executes the **actual production JS intro renderer and intro geometry fitter** with synthetic Typewriter/Keyboard stage widths and heights, and validates no verse-size coupling, identical fitted size at identical dimensions across synthetic Acts 5:29/16:31, fully reserved two-line text throughout animation, reasonable width fill/no overflow, rotation/font hooks, and unchanged timing. Existing `scripts/validate_read_my_verse.js` asserts independent intro sizing; CI retains all R01–R12/N7 audio recovery and production Web build. **R12b iPhone NOT TESTED.** Real iPhone WebKit font metrics, landscape and screenshots still require owner's physical observation. Verify Acts 5:29 and Acts 16:31 Typewriter show **two lines “Tap to type” / “the verse.”** at the same comfortable width, with no third line or clipping; check Keyboard, portrait/rotation, ghost-letter positioning, original per-verse text layout and R12 opening-quote/typed B/audio chunk mapping after intro. No AVAudioSession/category, Apple Music, AirPods, Welcome Back, Flashcards, other games, profile or private voice changes. Stable `ios-prep`/`main` unmodified; no automated merge/promote. Original production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` has **10 private Zookeepers**; separate Audio Test `.audiotest` has **11 different profiles**. Maintain *separate* off-device private backups, require explicit owner approval before any production-ID install, never uninstall/reset/import/restore/upload voice or profiles. **R13 `PLAY-02` Ghost Writer picker flash stays a separate follow-up, not bundled.**

## HISTORICAL R12 / READ-01 — opening quotation marks stay with next typed phrase ([PR #141](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/141))

**R11 / LEARN-01 owner physical-iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #140](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/140) merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `97d31507b22e92f541ec800e10b08801695ab22e`. The owner reports a real iPhone test and states it is **“doing everything you said it would.”** Mark the extra Learn Welcome Back busy-message bug **closed** after R01–R10 were already accepted. The exact iPhone installed source SHA, iOS version and optional route coverage were not provided; do not claim additional scenarios passed. Earlier R11 `NOT TESTED` text under the *historical R11 heading* below describes pre-acceptance status only.

**Original R12 / `READ-01` owner observation:** In Read My Verse **Typewriter** and possibly **Keyboard**, the spoken first chunk is **“They replied”**, but the first displayed/typed chunk finishes **“They replied, ‘”**, incorrectly leaving the *opening* quotation mark with words that precede the quoted speech. The canonical example is **“They replied, ‘Believe in the Lord Jesus…’”**. This is distinct from the obsolete pre-picker `READ-ACCESS` testing obstacle, already resolved by PR #124. No need to reopen a child-facing DEV tester.

**Source-supported cause:** `read_my_verse.js` has `buildDisplayChunks(verseText, echoParts)`, which locates each spoken `echoParts` phrase inside canonical Scripture and forms each display chunk from one phrase start **through the next phrase start**. The punctuation and whitespace preceding the next phrase are therefore included at the end of the previous *visual* chunk, including an opening quote. The original `echoParts` narration index and the actual audio content are not wrong; the mismatch is display-only. Do not alter Scripture data, recorded speech, MP3 file paths or native audio ownership to address it.

**Focused [R12 PR #141](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/141), `fix/r12-read-typing-opening-quote-boundary` → integration only:** The original `buildDisplayChunks` stays **unchanged** for all activities. For **Typewriter and Keyboard only**, if the ordinary mapped chunks reconstruct the **exact canonical `verseText`**, `groupTypingOpeningQuotes` shifts an unambiguously opening curly quote (`‘`, `“`, etc.) or a **contextually opening** straight ASCII quote (`'`, `"`) plus its existing adjacent whitespace from the preceding visual chunk to the beginning of the next typed phrase. It leaves commas, closing curly quotes, word-ending straight apostrophes, contractions, unquoted boundaries and already-correct `echoParts` untouched. It neither adds nor discards one character: `chunks.join("") === verseText`. The quote is visible at the start of the *next* typed chunk via existing `getInitialRevealCount`; the first child tap still types the next **letter**, without requiring a punctuation tap. If `echoParts` fails to map to canonical Scripture, leave the older fallback alone instead of inventing a boundary. **All original numbered narration indices `a`, `b`, ... remain the same**; R12 does not modify chunk completion clocks, iPhone interruption recovery, audio files or scoring. The DEV forced-short/long mode still chooses its original narrated segment based on unmodified chunk lengths. **Tap Words in Order, Unscramble, Crawl, Balloons, Fish and Chunk Sequence are not changed** by this visual grouping.

**Automated and physical acceptance:** New `scripts/validate_r12_read_typing_opening_quotes.js` is wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` and runs the **actual production Read My Verse module** on synthetic examples covering original `They replied, ‘Believe...`, curly/straight single and double quotes, paired closers, final apostrophe, contraction, nested quotes, unmatched fallback and no-quote text. Tests validate canonical joined text, unchanged `audioIndices` / `getChunkAudioPath` suffixes, first-letter tap / no extra punctuation tap, both Typewriter and Keyboard, unchanged non-typing activity chunks and original forced-short/long test-mode selection. Preserve N7/R01–R11 and production Web build. **R12 owner physical iPhone: NOT TESTED.** Source/unit tests do not prove actual iPhone rendering or audio sync. [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) covers Review → Learn → Listen Activities → Typewriter and Keyboard, first/next chunk visual boundaries, exact verse, correct spoken `a`/`b` order, punctuation-only/quote cases, interruption checkpoint and no other Read regressions. Original production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` has **10 private Zookeepers** and separate Audio Test bundle **11 different profiles**; protect independently backed up private data. No uninstall/reset/restore/import or profile/voice uploads; production-ID device install requires separate owner authorization. No auto-merge or stable `ios-prep`/`main` promotion. **R13 `PLAY-02`** is next only after owner acceptance of R12.

**R10 / READ-04 OWNER PHYSICAL IPHONE PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #139](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/139) was merged into integration at `6fbeb125c792a46845e2ab9a4992827bd53c08a4`. Two independently live paragraphs announce the same progress. Source proves this UI duplication mechanism, not that audio/narration recovery itself is broken.

**[R11 PR #140](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/140), `fix/r11-learn-welcome-back-duplicate-busy-status` → integration only:** While a Learn recovery attempt is busy and the shared progress slot exists, keep Learn's separate status paragraph **empty and hidden**. A genuine failed narration verification still shows **“Sound isn't ready yet. Please try again.”** under an enabled **Try again!** button. A new tap clears the stale error. If shared markup is unavailable, the local fallback displays only a simple **“Checking sound...”** status while its button is disabled. The shared Welcome Back helper/CSS, Zoo Escape, other overlays, audio session, Apple Music/AirPods takeover, Learn Menu/HIDE active-time clocks, mute, Read/Flashcards/Feeding, Web audio policy, scores and profiles remain unchanged.

**Verification and owner gate:** `scripts/validate_r11_learn_welcome_back_busy_status.js` runs the **actual production Learn sync function** and **actual shared Welcome Back helper** in a synthetic DOM, covering initial action, single rotating accessible busy phrase, idempotent timer, disabled action, genuine error and retry, stale-error clearing, verified success hiding, menu and local fallback. CI also retains N7/R01–R10, Swift grammar and production Web build. ## HISTORICAL R10 / READ-04 — relative native Read word/melody volume ([PR #139](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/139))

**R09b / FLASH-05 owner physical-iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #138](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/138) was merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `9abff454e34e2068f6051f09b8958c67731d72e4`. Owner **merged, physically tested** R09b and reports recorded-voice playback volume is **pretty good**, explicitly authorizing us to **mark R09/FLASH-05 complete**. This closes R09 and its earlier insufficient #137 candidate; it does **not** mean the original #137 alone was accepted. R01–R08 were already owner-reported iPhone PASS. The exact locally installed SHA, iOS version and unreported optional playback routes were not supplied; do not infer full-matrix testing. Earlier R09b `NOT TESTED` paragraphs below are **historical**, not current status.

**R10 owner symptom, not calibrated audio measurement:** `READ-04` says **Tap in Order** and **Unscramble** word beeps and little melodies sounded quieter than other games' Practice/Playground feedback at similar device volume. Their native output is `BibloZooNativeReadOutputPlugin` in `ios/App/App/SceneDelegate.swift`, not `BibloZooNativeSoundPlugin.swift`. The native fixed `wavData` voices used **0.22 peak gain** per correct note, **0.11 wrong**, **0.08 optional negative**, while some other native game sound recipes generate stronger peaks or mix more notes. For example, Tower of Bible correct tone's 0.07 authored per-note amplitude uses a 3.40 source multiplier in its independent native PCM synthesizer. This is a source-supported **relative-gain hypothesis**, not a speaker/AirPods loudness or perceived-dB measurement. Web Read also has an authored 0.22 peak and keeps it unchanged. Do not raise every app sound or change hardware volume based on this observation.

**Focused R10 [PR #139](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/139), branch `fix/r10-read-native-cue-loudness-balance` → integration only:** Increase native Read **correct single-word** order/scramble feedback **0.22 → 0.32** (roughly +3.3 dB source amplitude). Set **each note of a four-note completion** to a gentler **0.28**, since completion voices overlap; set wrong Order feedback to **0.13** (previous 0.11, still softer), and keep optional native negative at **0.08**. DEV-only waveform Lab audition aligns with child volumes (**0.32 single, 0.28 completion**) and keeps matched gain between waveforms. Existing native 44.1 kHz mono 16-bit synthesis, original scale/pitches, individual 0.17/0.2s note lengths, attack/release, precise **0/90/100/180/190/270/300ms** authored note spacing, R08 immediate-first/live-shared-clock scheduling, delegate completion and 12-voice cap stay intact. No Read narration, Crawl music, generated typing-key sounds, Web/PWA/Android gains, other games, Flashcards mic/leveling, mute/Start gesture policy, AVAudioSession, Apple Music/AirPods/held-switcher recovery, score or profile changes.

**Automation and owner gate:** New `scripts/validate_r10_read_native_cue_loudness.js`, wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`, compiles and executes the **actual Swift native Read WAV synthesis function** with synthetic samples. It verifies the source waveform's 0.32 / 0.28 / 0.13 / 0.08 peaks, correct vs previous 0.22 source RMS, and bounded two-/four-note overlap below 0.66 amplitude on synthetic mixes; asserts unchanged authored timing, R08 cancel/readiness, DEV comparison and Web original authoring. The existing R08 and N7f2 validators are updated **only for expected deliberately changed native gains** and continue to enforce source clock/timbre/safety constraints. The rest of N7/R01–R09 and production Web build remain required. These Swift/Linux tests are **not** Apple SDK linking, acoustic measurements or proof of what a child hears. **R10 physical iPhone: NOT TESTED.** Review and separately approve any production-ID device install after private backup. Compare **five correct Tap in Order taps, wrong taps, completion flourish, two-note Unscramble word confirmation, four-note finish**, at fixed iPhone system volume with Practice/Playground beeps and original Read narration on **speaker and AirPods**; check no harsh/distorted or startlingly loud reward, no silent first tap after Apple Music/held App Switcher + real Welcome Back, no compressed/rushed melody, ghost notes, skipped scoring or mute regression. Preserve separate original production **10-Zookeeper** backup vs **11-profile Audio Test** backup; no uninstall/reset/restore/import or voice/profile upload. Owner must report R10 PASS/FAIL separately before closing; don't automatically promote to stable `ios-prep` or `main`. **Next separate roadmap item: R11 `LEARN-01` duplicate Welcome Back status.**

## HISTORICAL R09b / FLASH-05 — audible native dry voice and responsive 16-bar mic meter ([PR #138](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/138))

**New physical-iPhone owner result for the merged R09 [#137](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/137): FOLLOW-UP NEEDED / AUDIBILITY GOAL NOT MET.** R09 #137 merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `829e8f1f97e00cb07b23b630fa735ac1e5130705`. Owner reported that **even speaking quite loudly, native Flashcards recorded-voice playback only just about matched the narrated instructions**; while speaking loudly, the 16-bar recording meter rose only to **about 50%**. This is a real-device observation, not a calibrated dB comparison, a broken microphone finding, a Wacky effect regression, or owner acceptance of R09. **FLASH-05 remains OPEN.** All R01–R08 are previously owner-reported physical-iPhone PASS; do not change their acceptance.

**Source-based explanation:** Native Flashcards records raw mono RAM-only Float32 PCM under `AVAudioSession.playAndRecord/.measurement`, while Web uses browser MediaRecorder and instruction prompts are fixed prerecorded MP3s. R09 #137 applied at most 1.8× dry makeup and could refuse a whole quiet take due to a single sharp peak. The old native live RMS-to-meter formula `(dBFS + 55)/55` shows **50% at roughly -27.5 dBFS RMS**; it is not a clipping meter and its 50% does not mean sound is being recorded at half amplitude. The precise on-device mic and playback level differences are unknown; do not assert broken hardware, require the meter reach 100%, or change mic AGC/.measurement mode/audio-session policies based on the bar display.

**Focused [R09b PR #138](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/138), branch `fix/r09b-flashcards-voice-leveling-and-meter` → integration ONLY, iPhone NOT TESTED:** On the existing capture worker, retain only full-take RMS/peak coarse numeric read-only diagnostics and a measured **1.0–3.2×** maximum dry voice makeup factor (up to ~10 dB) when there is enough active speech and no near-silent/noisy/already-loud source. One high consonant no longer vetoes a quiet take. During the **child's explicit normal Play My Recording gesture**, apply a **~12ms attack / 180ms release voice activity envelope**, blend boost back down across silent gaps, and apply **smooth approximately 2:1 peak compression above 0.22** with ~80ms gain-release and a final **0.84 full-scale peak ceiling** in a **temporary dry-only output PCM buffer**. This is intentionally mild and may still need a real-device refinement; it does not make a calibrated loudness claim. The original saved RAM take remains byte-for-byte unchanged (including repeated Play), and all original Wacky/chorus/DEV effect presets still process original PCM. The microphone capture input, countdown, `.measurement` capture mode, `.soloAmbient` output policy, AVAudioSession priority and interruption/route lifecycle, child UI progression, Web/PWA/Android, voice privacy, profile stores and scoring are untouched. For native ONLY, rescale display-only live 16-bar RMS meter to `(dBFS+50)/35`: the old 50% input shows **~64% (10 of 16 bars)** and the upper bars respond more naturally without faking volume or changing PCM.

**Automated and physical gate:** Existing `scripts/validate_r09_flashcards_measured_dry_level.js` is updated to compile and execute the **actual production Swift level-estimator and normal dry render/copy code** with synthetic soft, normal, loud, peak-heavy, silent/noisy, long-gap, short/invalid and repeated takes; confirms output ceiling, no repeat gain compounding, Wacky unchanged and native meter re-scale. CI retains N7c–N7m, R01–R08, Swift parse and production Web build. **Synthetic Linux tests cannot prove Apple SDK compilation, iPhone speaker/AirPods loudness, real children speaking, or parity with narration. R09b owner iPhone: NOT TESTED.** [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) checks a safe adult test phrase spoken softly and normally at fixed mic distance and device volume, relative to nearby narrated instructions on speaker and AirPods; no hiss/crackle/pumping/clipped consonants, unchanged Wacky effects, stable repeated Play, 16-bar responsiveness (not 100% demand), interruptions, app switching, route changes and R01–R08. **FLASH-05 is not closed until the owner reports comfortable actual iPhone voice playback.** Next distinct roadmap step remains **R10 `READ-04`** after acceptance; stable `ios-prep` and `main` are untouched. Production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` holds **10 private Zookeepers** and separate Audio Test has **11 different** profiles: keep independent private off-device backups, no uninstall/reset/restore/import, no voice/profile uploads or telemetry, and separately authorize any production-ID installation.

## HISTORICAL R09 / FLASH-05 — measured, bounded native Flashcards dry replay level (PR #137; iPhone NOT TESTED)

**R08 owner physical-iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #136](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/136) merged to `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` as `d4714f0d4aa7db9bc3af17d047bba7f6ff6e4ca9`. The owner reports both Tap in Order (`READ-05`) and Unscramble (`READ-06`) now **work great** on their physical iPhone. R01–R07 were already owner accepted. Exact locally installed SHA/iOS version and full optional matrix weren't supplied. The earlier R08 `NOT TESTED` text below predates this report.

**R09 / `FLASH-05` owner observation, not a calibrated measurement:** Flashcards' native dry voice recording sounded substantially quieter than the earlier Web recording. The browser path uses `getUserMedia({audio:true})` and MediaRecorder; native `BibloZooMicSessionProbePlugin` retains unprocessed mono Float PCM while `AVAudioSession` uses `.playAndRecord/.measurement`, and the child's dry `playCapture` copied the PCM unchanged to `AVAudioPlayerNode`. [Apple's measurement-mode documentation](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfaudio/avaudiosession/mode-swift.struct/measurement) notes that the mode reduces system signal/dynamics processing and can lower output levels. That is a plausible explanation, **not proof** the owner's recording is quieter because of that setting, its input route, hardware gain or output. Do not change capture session category, system volume, `.soloAmbient` output, microphone processing or route without separate evidence.

**[R09 PR #137](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/137), `fix/r09-flashcards-measured-dry-replay-level` → integration only:** At normal completed capture, on the native **capture worker only**, compute full-file RMS/true peak and a speech-level activity gate from the already-retained RAM samples. The optional dry-playback gain is applied **only if the take is objectively quiet**, has enough above-floor signal, no nonfinite samples and no high transient; gain is limited to **1.0–1.8×** (maximum +5.1 dB) *and* the original true peak times gain may not exceed **0.84 full scale**. During the next deliberately child-tapped **Play My Recording**, apply this scalar solely to a new, bounded, output-side PCM buffer, with a 0.84 defensive sample clamp. The in-RAM saved recording is never altered, replay never compounds, all Wacky/chorus/DEV effects use the original PCM, silence/noise/loud or clipped takes stay at gain 1. No JS voice bytes, files, uploads, retained child recordings, audio-session changes, auto-start, volume slider, capture-meter behavior, scoring or profile writes. The existing read-only native `getCaptureState` exposes only rounded integer `rmsDbFS`, `peakDbFS` and `dryReplayGain` for optional owner-level numeric diagnostics; these reset on discard/next take and are not shown to the child or logged.

**Verification and decision gate:** `scripts/validate_r09_flashcards_measured_dry_level.js` compiles/runs the **actual Swift level estimator and original dry PCM copy path** with synthetic sine speech/noise/silence/hot transient/invalid samples, repeated dry replay and unchanged Wacky PCM; wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` with R01–R08, N7c/D, Swift parse, safety/privacy and production Web build. A Linux Swift mock is **not** actual iPhone AGC, speaker/AirPods gain, Apple SDK compilation or objective native-versus-Web comparison. **R09 owner iPhone acceptance: NOT TESTED.** [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) requires matched phrase, mic distance, system volume and route on native Flashcards versus Web/Safari plus normal/loud/quiet/no speech, AirPods/speaker, repeated Play, effects, interruption, level/clipping and profiles. If gain is ineffective or amplifies noise, don't mark fixed or silently raise gain; report for an isolated refinement. **R10 `READ-04`** is the next separate roadmap slice once R09 is accepted. Original production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` has **10 private Zookeepers**, separate Audio Test app has **11**; keep independent private off-device backups, don't uninstall/import/reset or share child voice/private JSON; **separately authorize any production-ID phone install**. Stable `ios-prep` and `main` untouched.

## HISTORICAL R08 / READ-05 + READ-06 — post-interruption native Read cue timing ([PR #136](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/136))

**R07 iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** Owner reports merged [PR #135](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/135), integration merge `c040dcaa64132e876f554472abedc9470ddf069d`, is **working as intended on their real iPhone** for both Balloons (`READ-02`) and Fish (`READ-03`). R01–R06 had already been owner-accepted. The actual locally installed SHA/iOS version and optional full matrix are unknown. All old R07 "NOT TESTED" statements below are **historical**; do not infer READ-05/06 pass.

**Next issue R08 / `READ-05` + `READ-06`:** After Apple Music/AirPods or a switcher interruption, **Tap in Order** can have no native cue for the first four of five word taps until the fifth, and **Unscramble** can play its normal two-note melody almost simultaneously. `read_my_verse.js` already routes these through `BibloZooNativeReadOutputController.playCue` after the real child gesture, with lifecycle epoch invalidation; native iOS forbids WebAudio/HTML fallback. The **actual Swift cue owner** is `BibloZooNativeReadOutputPlugin.playCue` in `ios/App/App/SceneDelegate.swift`. It scheduled the zero-delay **first note** via `player.play(atTime: player.deviceCurrentTime + 0)`; Apple's `play(atTime:)` requires a **strictly future device time**, which may reset to 0 after all players stop. It also sampled each not-yet-playing player's device clock separately for the remaining 90–300ms offsets, and removed pending scheduled players from `cues` using `!isPlaying`. Those are demonstrable source timing hazards and a plausible cause, but **not an independently reproduced real-device root cause**. Apple references: [play(atTime:)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfaudio/avaudioplayer/play(atTime:)) and [deviceCurrentTime](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfaudio/avaudioplayer/devicecurrenttime).

**Focused [R08 PR #136](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/136) → integration ONLY:** Pre-create and `prepareToPlay()` the full bounded fixed-note cue, then call **immediate `first.player.play()`** after the existing real iOS Start/Let's go session claim. For later notes use one `anchorTime = first.player.deviceCurrentTime` (now running) plus the *unchanged authored* 90/100/180/190/270/300ms offsets. Do not prune `!isPlaying` future notes; preserve their strong ownership until native delegate completion, 12-voice cap, interruption/Exit/`stopCues`. A failed first play rejects the pattern without a delayed ghost cue. DEV-only waveform audition uses the same timing owner to preserve future R10 comparisons; no changed pitch, PCM envelope, gain, loudness or sound policy. No new audio activation, AVAudioSession category, autoplay, fallback, Swift mic/recording code, profile/score changes or Web/PWA/Android behavior.

**Regression and owner gate:** `scripts/validate_r08_read_native_cue_timing.js` is wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` to **compile and run the actual extracted Swift cue helper** against mock player clocks starting at zero, verify five separate order notes, an Unscramble duet at +100ms, a four-note flourish, rejected/partial failure without queued ghost audio, and VM-execute the actual JS native adapter under stale/new lifecycle epochs. Preserve all N7f/N7f2/N7m, Read, R01–R07, Swift parse and Web build checks. **R08 physical iPhone owner acceptance: NOT TESTED for Tap in Order and NOT TESTED for Unscramble.** Follow [TESTING.md](TESTING.md): repeat five-word and paired-note cues after 10–15 seconds AirPods Apple Music / held App Switcher, successful single Welcome Back, failed readiness and second interruption, no delayed burst/unearned score, original completion melody/speaker/AirPods, original Web output. Linux Swift mock/grammar is not Apple SDK compilation or proof of real audible speaker output. **Next after R08 acceptance: R09 `FLASH-05`**, separate measurement-first native voice level investigation. Stable `ios-prep`/`main` untouched; original production bundle has **10 private Zookeepers**, separate Audio Test has **11**, backups must not be combined. Production-ID device install needs **separate owner approval** and private off-device backup; no uninstall/reset/import/upload of child voices, profiles or family JSON (family-v2 omits separate Zoo rescue stats).

I think we can mark this one fixed.”** Mark R05 `DAILY-01` and its bundled ordinary Missing Words menu pause **FIXED / OWNER PHYSICAL IPHONE PASS**; R04 `LEARN-02` held-switcher fix and R01–R03 were already accepted. **Active next issue R06 / `PLAY-01`:** In **Playground → Verse Typer**, while the caterpillar is traversing/typing the **Bible book name** and its head/segments/tail are pulsing, open the native iOS **App Switcher and hold it open** for 10–15 seconds without selecting another app. Owner observed the caterpillar head/lines changing color in the still-visible thumbnail. Expect **the identical motion/color frame to freeze immediately on UIKit inactive**, regardless of whether WKWebView sets `document.hidden`. On return, keep one existing Welcome Back until the child's deliberate **Let's go!** succeeds; restore the same word/caterpillar position, no skipped letters, bonus, timer, score, sound or hidden completion. Owner only observed continuing **visual changes**; whether scoring advanced in the original defect is **not proven**. R07 `READ-02/READ-03` remains the next separate priority after R06, not bundled.

**R06 source-supported diagnosis:** Existing `verse_games/shared/native-verse-typer-audio.js` already subscribes to Swift `biblozooAppLifecycle(active:false)` at `willResignActive` and informs Verse Typer's `onAppInactive`, which calls the existing `playgroundWelcome.pauseFromNativeInactive()`; `onAppActive` reveals the same return gate. `verse_playground/verse_typer/game.js pauseTyperForReturn()` already sets `returnPaused=true`, `state.paused=true`, and `welcomeClock.pause()`, so its `trackedTimeout` deadlines and keyboard handler are already gated. **But** `game.css` has independent compositor CSS keyframes `vt-tail-color-pulse-*`, `vt-tail-rainbow`, `vt-ripple-wiggle`, `vt-crawl-in/out`, sparkles, cocoon/butterfly and HUD animations; those advance even when the JavaScript clock is paused and WebKit's thumbnail remains visible. Queued `requestAnimationFrame` caterpillar position/entrance callbacks can also execute after UIKit inactivity without checking `returnPaused`. The observed visual drift follows directly from that gap. Do not replace the working audio/session/clock ownership or assert unobserved scoring corruption.

**Focused [R06 PR #134](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/134) candidate `fix/r06-playground-verse-typer-visual-freeze` → integration only:** `pauseTyperForReturn()` now captures only **currently-running** `#app.getAnimations({subtree:true})` CSS animations and WAAPI-backed transitions, calls `animation.pause()` safely, and adds scoped `html.vt-return-visual-paused #app ... animation-play-state: paused !important` so newly painted keyframes also freeze. The original animation handles/currentTime are preserved; **no CSS animation resets, class removal or new audio**. An actual successful `continueTyperAfterReturn()` after a child gesture, and (in native mode) positive `nativeTyperAudio.isReady()`, unfreezes `welcomeClock`, removes class, and resumes those **same** running players without resuming preexisting authored-paused players; a failed/denied native resume keeps both time and visuals paused. Pending rAF repositions are dropped while inactive and retried after resume; pending travel entrance is deferred with the existing pause-aware `trackedTimeout`. Stop/Back/mode navigation clears the scoped visual pause. Existing native `willResignActive` listener, Welcome Back owner, native 19-PCM ID bank, AVAudioSession categories, score/rewards and Web/PWA/Android audio remain untouched. CSS visual freezing also follows the existing browser visibility controller.

**R06 test gate:** New `scripts/validate_r06_playground_verse_typer_visual_freeze.js` in `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` VM-executes the real Verse Typer native owner callbacks, tracked gameplay timeout, visuals and deferred RAF. It asserts UIKit inactive while WebKit remains visible freezes tail/head/color/travel animation player timestamps and score/word count for 13.5 seconds, duplicate inactive is idempotent, active alone never resumes, native readiness refusal retains Welcome Back, only real child resume restores original frame and remaining active deadline, a second interruption works, and non-native visibility still pauses. Keep N6n Verse Typer audio, Playground Patch67/68, R01–R05 and production Web build green. Tests/Swift syntax on Linux cannot prove physical iOS compositor or full Xcode SDK. **R06 iPhone acceptance: NOT TESTED**; owner review/merge/real-device validation is required before closing. ## HISTORICAL R05 / DAILY-01 + small R04 Learn-menu follow-up (pre-owner acceptance, 2026-11-17)

**R04 / `LEARN-02` OWNER-REPORTED PHYSICAL IPHONE PASS / CLOSED:** [PR #132](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/132) merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `4f8808c1a231ed8afb85ccb60063f90dc319d913`. Owner explicitly says **“Everything about that PR is working.”** The held App Switcher freeze, Welcome Back and previously requested R04 scope are accepted. **Separate newly discovered observation:** while the **Say the Verse Out Loud** prompt is pulsing and its timer running, opening the **in-app Learn Menu** must pause timer/animation; the earlier R04 implementation did not. The owner expressly asks to bundle this small `LEARN-MENU-PAUSE` follow-up with R05, **not reopen completed `LEARN-02` or create a stand-alone PR**. Exact installed SHA/OS remain unreported. R01–R03 were already owner-confirmed fixed. Do not promote `ios-prep` or `main` or imply unrelated iPhone checks passed.

**Next roadmap issue [R05 PR #133](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/133) / `DAILY-01` (candidate, owner iPhone NOT TESTED):** In the ordinary Daily Questions flow, open **Check the Verse → Listen to the Verse**, then enter and hold the iOS App Switcher. Native voice stops, but **no Welcome Back** appears and the bottom disabled button remains **“Listening…”**. Owner's prior workaround was closing/reopening the verse panel. Expected: a genuine interruption stops the current native voice, resets the button to **Listen to the Verse**, displays **one existing Welcome Back**, preserves the question, and resumes only on an explicit new Listen tap after **Let's go!**; no false question/reward credit. Question **Play Sound** and Check Verse voice are different sources and must be retested separately.

**R05 diagnosed source paths:** (1) `daily_questions.js`'s pre-existing Patch 71 `dailyQuestionReturn` listens only to document visibility/pagehide via `welcome-back-return.js`; when UIKit resigns active but WKWebView is still visible, Swift's existing `biblozooAppLifecycle(active:false)` never reaches the Daily controller. (2) Shared native `app.js playVerseDetailListen()` intentionally accepted `Screen.VERSE_DETAIL` and `Screen.FLASHCARDS` but **not `Screen.DAILY_SESSION`** for native `completed` or `interrupted` events: a valid native Daily ref→whole-verse can remain in the apparent playing state even aside from task switching. (3) Daily's `finally` checked only the verse ID, so an old interrupted Promise resolving late could reset a newer Listen attempt on the same verse. These are concrete source defects, not evidence of physical-device retesting.

**R05 targeted implementation on `fix/r05-daily-check-verse-native-recovery-and-learn-menu-pause` → integration only:** Native-iOS-only `dailyQuestions.js` subscribes to the **existing** Swift lifecycle, routes `active:false/true` through its **existing** `pauseFromNativeInactive()/showAfterNativeActive()` controller, cancels the current shared native/HTML preview, invalidates its attempt generation, resets `verseAudioPlaying`, and repaints the button even if document.hidden is false. Its `onContinue()` only acknowledges/render-returns; **no audio is automatically started**. Source `onInterrupted` also triggers the same gate if Apple Music/remote Play interrupts while UIKit stays active. `app.js` now admits `DAILY_SESSION` as a valid active screen for native verse `completed` and `interrupted` callbacks. Async `finally` requires exact session **object and request token**, so a prior cancelled playback can never clear the new `Listening` state. Block Listen taps while Welcome Back is pending/hidden. Genuine missing-asset failure returns original explicit Listen retry with **no false Welcome Back**. Stop/Close/Exit invalidate old attempt and keep question/help state; never issue Daily credit from voice acknowledgment. No new Swift category/mic/recorder/WebAudio or change to browser/PWA/Android audio.

**Bundled `LEARN-MENU-PAUSE` source fix:** R04 already has `appReturnPausedAt`, `waitForActiveLearnTime`, HIDE CSS animation pause and WAAPI frame capture, but `openLearnMenu()` only paused narration. It didn't pause the Missing Words pulsing prompt, timer or transitions. Now Menu Open explicitly shares the same HIDE frozen checkpoint and pauses animations, while **Menu Close** shifts only the paused wall-clock duration and resumes that frame **without Welcome Back**. If UIKit also becomes inactive during an open menu, neither repeated pause destroys the original WAAPI frame nor can Menu Close bypass the separately pending native Welcome Back; if the child acknowledges while the menu remains open, the menu itself still holds the timer. All HIDE timed/zero-remaining waits honor the menu pause. Cleanup happens on navigation/Exit, no changed recorded profile or extra word removals. This local menu behavior applies to Web/PWA/Android as well; the native lifecycle binding itself remains iOS-only.

**Regression and owner gates:** `scripts/validate_r05_daily_check_verse_native_recovery.js` runs actual Daily controller/Listen click and shared native app.js preview in VM with visible WebKit, duplicate UIKit inactive, foreground native-only interruption, native completion, delayed stale Promise and true error/web fallback paths. `scripts/validate_r04_learn_menu_pause_followup.js` runs original menu Open/Close plus the Patch 71 HIDE return/timer/animation code with long menu hold, overlapping real UIKit interruption, multiple events and Web isolation. Both in `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` plus existing R01–R04/N7k/Patch71 and production Web build; Linux Swift grammar ≠ Xcode/iPhone hardware testing. See [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) for standalone **Daily native audio** and **Missing Words Menu** physical checks. The app thumbnail still visibly animates or advances, whereas gameplay/timing should freeze immediately and preserve the exact current word/position until a deliberate **Welcome Back → Let's go!**. Separate from R05 Daily Questions, R06 Playground Verse Typer, R07 Read Balloons/Fish and R11 duplicate Learn busy copy. No iPhone R04 retest yet.

**Source-confirmed lifecycle gap:** Native Swift `BibloZooNativeSoundPlugin.leftForeground()` already emits `biblozooAppLifecycle {active:false}` at `UIApplication.willResignActive`, even while WebKit remains `document.hidden=false`, and `returnedToForeground()` emits `{active:true}`. `app.js` already has the Patch 71 `appActivityReturn` Welcome Back controller for `Screen.HIDE` when `State.sayVerseActive`; `onPause` sets `appReturnPausedAt`, `getSayVersePct()` uses that frozen timestamp, `onContinue` shifts the clock by the paused duration, and `waitForActiveLearnTime()` checks `appActivityReturn.isPaused()`. **But the app-level controller only subscribed to `visibilitychange/pagehide/pageshow` through the shared controller**, not the native UIKit event. The **separate** native Learn listener only covers `CHUNKS` and `ECHO`, not Missing Words. When the held switcher leaves WebKit visible, neither handler pauses the HIDE controller; its timer, CSS breathing/transition animations and phase timers advance. This is a source-grounded explanation of the reported symptom, not an assertion of hardware retest.

**Focused R04 [PR #132](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/132), `fix/r04-learn-missing-words-held-switcher-pause` → `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` only:** Attach one guarded native-only iOS `biblozooAppLifecycle` listener to the **existing app activity Welcome Back**, scoped strictly to `Screen.HIDE`: on `active:false`, call `pauseFromNativeInactive()`; on `active:true`, call `showAfterNativeActive()`. The shared controller deduplicates visibility/native events, displays the original one-button Welcome Back, and **never releases gameplay before the actual Let's go! tap**. HIDE classification also covers the in-progress auto-start/fade transition, not idle/info screens; no false popup for a normal in-app Learn menu Close. Freeze running HIDE WAAPI animations (including CSS transitions) and `animation-play-state` for keyframes within the visible thumbnail; resume them on acknowledgement, clear on navigation/Back to Title. Replace the two HIDE wall-clock transition delays with `waitForActiveLearnTime`, gate the initial metadata-to-timer step on active status, and ensure even a zero-remaining timing loop waits for foreground/acknowledgment before mutating the phase. The existing paused timestamp is used to shift the say-verse deadline, so there is **no fast-forward, newly hidden word, accidental reward, silent auto-resume, media takeover or profile mutation**. No Swift/native audio category changes, timer/score changes in other activities or Web/PWA/Android lifecycle binding.

**Regression and review:** New `scripts/validate_r04_learn_missing_words_inactive_pause.js` in `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` VM-executes the actual `app.js` activity return logic, simulates native inactive while WebKit remains visible, verifies unchanged percentage/word count and animated frame through long waits, one Welcome Back and paused state on active return, and continuation only after user acknowledgment; also checks idle/navigation/Final Recall/Web isolation, Swift event source, CSS pause and transition/fade timing source contracts. Retain existing Patch 71, N7k, R01–R03, daily/audio and production Web checks. **Node/Swift parse/Web build cannot verify actual iOS App Switcher animation or Xcode SDK typechecking**; owner physical PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED remains **PENDING**. [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) documents exact iPhone steps, including switcher held 10+ seconds, phase and position preservation, second interruption, menu Close, other Learn modes and ten-profile safety.

**Project rules:** Create one reviewable PR only against current integration; `ios-prep` and `main` unchanged. Never automatically install production-ID app, uninstall or overwrite data. The original production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` has **10 private Zookeepers**, separate Audio Test `.audiotest` has **11**; retain off-device private export, do not upload voices, backups, profile identifiers or photos. Owner separately authorizes iPhone install and performs/records physical acceptance before closing R04. Next after R04 is **R05 / `DAILY-01`**, not part of this PR.

## HISTORICAL R03 CODE CANDIDATE — Flashcards AirPods-to-speaker recorded-voice replay (2026-11-18)

**Owner acceptance just received:** After merged [PR #129](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/129) and [PR #130](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/130), the owner explicitly states **both `FLASH-02` and `FLASH-03` are FIXED** from iPhone retesting. [PR #130](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/130) merged to `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `8e14c18b90cf8c4134f826d8e26479b507329e7b`. Do **not** keep their earlier "partial/pending" state as current. The exact physical-device build SHA, full optional route matrix and iOS version were not independently provided. `FLASH-01` remains owner-confirmed fixed. All other issue IDs are separate.

**Next roadmap item:** **R03 / `FLASH-04`**. Owner's prior physical-iPhone report: record a verse with AirPods microphone connected, finish a valid RAM-only take, then disconnect AirPods **before** **Play My Recording**; the voice is silent after the expected route switches to iPhone speaker. This problem is NOT the separate `FLASH-05` perceived volume difference. Scope: native Flashcards recorder `ios/App/App/SceneDelegate.swift`, JavaScript `verse_games/shared/native-flashcards-capture-n7c.js`, focused tests and handoff; Web/PWA/Android left untouched.

**Source-supported route race (credible candidate, awaiting iPhone confirmation):** In `BibloZooMicSessionProbePlugin.playCapture`, the old code copied recorded mono samples on `captureWorker`, constructed `AVAudioEngine`, connected `AVAudioPlayerNode` / all Wacky effects, **then** called `AVAudioSession.setActive(true)`. That can create the output graph against the stale inactive AirPods hardware/rate even though iOS updates the output route when the child's explicit Play reactivates the session. Swift didn't check that a current output exists; the worker's pending Play had no route-epoch fence. The route observer also ignored playback changes within the first 0.5 seconds, and it did not handle `AVAudioEngineConfigurationChange` (which Apple documents may stop and uninitialize the engine when hardware sample rate/channel changes). The bridge's `started:true` only indicates engine acceptance, **not heard audio**. This diagnosis is not yet proven on physical iPhone; do not claim that all silently rendered audio is now audible merely from Swift parsing/Node simulation.

**Narrow R03 implementation in [PR #131](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/131), `fix/r03-flashcards-airpods-route-replay` → `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` only:** Record the `playbackRouteEpoch` before asynchronous PCM copy and reject a stale request if an actual `.oldDeviceUnavailable/.newDeviceAvailable/.noSuitableRouteForCategory` notification occurs during that copy. Once the same original **real Play gesture** reaches main thread, call `session.setActive(true)` **before constructing the new engine**, check `session.currentRoute.outputs` nonempty and `engine.outputNode.outputFormat(forBus: 0)` has positive sample rate/channels, and fence output port/epoch again immediately before `engine.start()`. Use the active actual iOS hardware output; **do not force speaker**, override ports, change `.soloAmbient`, or schedule sound after returning from the background. Stop owned playback immediately if actual output port changes even within the 0.5s initial window, and observe only the owned engine's configuration reset on the main queue. A route refusal/reset returns an actionable **“Audio output changed. Tap Play to try again.”** and preserves the **same opaque, bounded native RAM take**, requiring another conscious Play tap. True `start-failed` keeps its original error; `playbackComplete` still requires matching native `.dataPlayedBack`, not engine acceptance; no fake grading/Next/Wacky unlock. Existing recording category handoff, audio priority, all ten Wacky presets and Web/PWA/Android contracts remain unchanged. No voice bytes, profiles or AirPods device identifiers enter JavaScript, GitHub, files or logs.

**Tests/evidence:** New `scripts/validate_r03_flashcards_route_replay.js` in `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` checks Swift source ordering/guards, output reconfiguration and route epoch, current-only native event completion, failed route retry and retained RAM take, plus preserved web recorder. Run existing R01/R02/N7c/N7d/N7d.2/N7e suites and production Web build. This is **Linux Swift syntax/source and JS VM** only; physical Xcode linking, actual speaker audibility and Bluetooth route changes are **NOT TESTED**. [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) records the separate iPhone speaker, AirPods, early disconnect and during-playback negative cases. The owner must approve updating the original production-ID app (`com.andydoane.biblozoo`) independently, retain private original **10 Zookeeper** production export, never import the separate **11-profile** Audio Test backup, and never uninstall/reset. Do not merge to `ios-prep`/`main`; PR review/merge is owner-controlled.

**R03 owner outcome:** NOT TESTED. No claims of a permanent solution until the owner confirms audible native replay on speaker, working explicit retry/no false completion, intact recording and prior R01–R02 behavior.

## HISTORICAL R02 BASE FIX — Flashcards Welcome Back on external audio (2026-11-17)

**MERGED [PR #129](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/129), owner partial pass with follow-up open, targeting `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` only:** R02 covers **`FLASH-02` and `FLASH-03`**, the physical-iPhone report that AirPods/Apple Music Play interrupts Flashcards instruction narration or **Listen to the Verse** and produces a red playback-error toast rather than the normal **Welcome Back**. This PR is not R03 Bluetooth recording-take route repair, R09 recording gain or R15 Silent Mode policy. **Hardware outcome NOT TESTED** for R02; only the owner can verify real Apple Music and AirPods behavior.

**Cause found in current source:** Swift's native AVAudioSession `.began` interruption calls `forgetPlayback()`, invalidates native narration and emits `biblozooAppLifecycle` with `reason:"audio-interruption"` even when UIKit stays `.active`. However, `flashcards/instruction-audio.js` flattened every non-ended `BibloZooNativeLearnTracks.play()` result into `"failed"` and displayed **“Instructions couldn't play”**. The Flashcards verse Listen path in `app.js` already distinguishes native `event.kind === "interrupted"` from `"failed"`, but returned only a boolean to `flashcards/flashcards.js`; its async button then displayed **“Audio couldn't play”**. Existing `BibloZooWelcomeBackReturn.attach` watched page visibility and native-inactive only when invoked by its owner, so a foreground-only AirPods media takeover could bypass the overlay.

**R02 change:** The instruction narrator now forwards *only* native `reason:"interrupted"` to its Flashcards owner before any error toast; missing files, decode errors and ordinary failed completions retain the existing retry. The app's native verse Preview accepts an **optional** `onInterrupted` callback for Flashcards (Web/PWA/Android callers and ordinary Verse Detail remain unchanged); its native interruption event calls the Flashcards owner before completing a failed Listen promise. Flashcards routes these signals to the **existing** `pauseFromNativeInactive()` / `showAfterNativeActive()` Welcome Back, with an additional guarded listener for Swift's explicit `biblozooAppLifecycle {active:false,reason:"audio-interruption"}` when an instruction/verse is actually in progress. Normal route/foreground notifications, screen navigation and failed files do not create false prompts. Pausing revokes the pending Listen generation, stops only owner audio, closes stale toasts and keeps verse, difficulty and progress intact. **Let's go! acknowledges and closes Welcome Back but never starts narration automatically**; the child deliberately taps **Try Instructions Again** or **Listen** to replay. No JS/Swift session activation, category, WebAudio fallback, mic format, voice storage or reward change.

**Regression and acceptance:** `scripts/validate_r02_flashcards_welcome_back.js` (wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`) tests genuine native instruction interruption vs missing-file error, one shared Welcome Back for both paths, no stale Listen toast, Swift event fallback, real file failure retry, completion gating and Web/PWA isolation. Node/production web CI and source tests **cannot** prove iPhone audible sound. [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) records exact owner AirPods/Apple Music reproduction steps, both distinct playback contexts, prompt/Retry/Back, avoiding music reclaim, no duplicate/ghost prompts, and 10-profile safety. **Owner physical PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED remains PENDING.** Never merge to `ios-prep` or `main`, reinstall the production-ID app without separately authorized owner consent, or claim R03/R04 fixed.

**R01 outcome immediately before this PR:** Owner explicitly reports **PR #128 merged, personally tested on physical iPhone and FLASH-01 fixed**. Integration post-merge commit `0246d775e7`, [Actions run 44194453573](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44194453573) green. Record this as a targeted owner-reported R01 PASS, not a blanket audio/interruption PASS. R02 is next; R03–R17 remain independently gated.

## CURRENT CODE FIX — R01 Flashcards UI pops after recording (2026-11-17)

**HISTORICAL R01 CODE PR — [#128](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/128) MERGED; owner has now physically tested and reports `FLASH-01` FIXED:** Branch `fix/r01-flashcards-ui-pops-after-recording` targets **only** `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` (not stable `ios-prep`/`main`). Owner requested a focused fix to `FLASH-01`: native UI button pops become silent after recording in Flashcards and remain silent on Home. This fix is NOT the AirPods/Apple Music interruption bug `FLASH-02/03`, silent route-changed recording `FLASH-04`, or level issue `FLASH-05`, all still open.

**Root cause established in code (device confirmation still pending):** `verse_games/shared/native-flashcards-capture-n7c.js` calls `BibloZooNativeSound.devPrepareForMicrophone()` after a real Record permission request. Its Swift implementation sets a synthetic mic handoff gate, calls `forgetPlayback()`, and drains the shared native PCM bank before the `.soloAmbient` → `.playAndRecord` transition. That bank includes `ui-pop-1/2`. The *independent* `verse_games/shared/native-ui-audio.js` controller was still `ready=true` from an earlier tap; no UIKit background/visibility event necessarily occurs during in-app recording, so later Home/Flashcards UI taps incorrectly go directly to `play()` and never call `preload()` again. Swift denies the no-longer-prepared sample; no browser fallback exists, by design. This is a state-readiness mismatch, not evidence of a damaged recording.

**Precise R01 patch:** Right **before** the existing mic output barrier, after permission is granted, `native-flashcards-capture-n7c.js` calls `root.BibloZooNativeUiAudio?.invalidate?.()` (default `stop:false`). This clears only stale UI ready/preparing/generation; it **does not** issue `setActive`, stop unrelated banks, change audio category, begin playback, or try to resume/steal Apple Music. The next eligible **real tap** invokes the existing bounded native `activateFromGesture({purpose:"ui"})` / `preload(ui-pop-1/2)` / `play` chain. Permission denial avoids the barrier and leaves already prepared effects. JS remains isolated to native-only iOS; original Web/PWA/Android recorder remains unchanged.

**Regression:** New `scripts/validate_r01_flashcards_ui_pop_handoff.js` in `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` executes the actual original recording, native recorder and UI-pop JS controllers with a fake native PCM bank that is removed by the mic barrier. It asserts both pops replay after a subsequent genuine tap, no passive activation/re-preload on Stop, RAM take retained and denial unaffected. Keep all existing N5a/N7d.2 capture and ownership tests. **CI PASS:** [GitHub Actions run 44191269823](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44191269823) on PR #128's initial code+docs revision completed **Node regression checks PASS** (including the new R01 controller/PCM-barrier test) and **Production web build PASS**. Recheck the latest run after any final docs-only commit. Neither this simulation nor Swift source parsing proves audio was heard on owner's iPhone.

**Owner device acceptance (see [TESTING R01 section](TESTING.md)):** after the owner reviews/merges and **explicitly authorizes** updating production-ID BibloZoo on original iPhone with private ten-profile production-family backup retained, listen to normal UI pops before Record, record/stop once, try eligible Flashcards taps and Home menu taps, repeat a second take, confirm **persistent pops restored** without phantom/doubled sounds, no automatic external music reclaim, real audio capture/replay still works and all **10 expected production Zookeepers** plus progress/pets remain. Some controls deliberately carry `data-no-ui-sound` (Record/Stop/Play); check ordinary navigation. If warmup makes the very first tap late/silent, record that observation separately; persistent post-record silence should be gone. **Real-device result PENDING; do not mark R01 resolved yet.**

**Post-merge correction (newer than the original R01 PR text above):** Owner merged PR #128, retested the UI pops on physical iPhone and now considers FLASH-01 fixed. The exact hardware build SHA and full recording/route matrix were not separately supplied. R02 is the active next PR; stable `ios-prep` and `main` still remain unchanged, and no backups/recordings were committed.

Exact first failing assertion was `scripts/validate_patch72_1c_whole_app_zoo_escape.js:46` → `AssertionError: fresh PWA cache generation`. The app deliberately changed `service-worker.js` from the old `2026_Nov_15_patch73_...` cache name to `2026_Nov_17_review_listen_activities_freeplay` to refresh all ten child Listen icons; six historical Zoo Escape/Feeding-related test scripts erroneously hardcoded a requirement that every cache version name contain `patch72_1` or `patch73`.

**Repair PR:** [#125](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/125), `fix/ci-cache-version-zoo-regressions` → **integration branch only**. Six tests updated: `validate_patch72_1c_whole_app_zoo_escape.js`, `validate_patch72_1c1_whole_app_test_harness.js`, `validate_patch72_1c2_identity_artwork_exit.js`, `validate_patch72_1c3_zoo_escape_sounds.js`, `validate_patch72_1d_zookeeper_stats.js`, and `validate_remove_hero_white_feed_check.js` (the sixth stale `patch73` assertion exposed after the first five passed). The previously fixed `validate_patch72_1b_feeding_zoo_escape.js` already uses the generic pattern. ## CURRENT FEATURE PR — Review → Learn / Listen Activities (2026-11-17, owner iPhone verification pending)

This is an approved product feature so children can freely enjoy Read My Verse and the owner can run all eight iPhone audio tests without developer menus. **No new Xcode install or physical iPhone acceptance is claimed by this PR.**

- **Home behavior:** If the current verse is not learned, its **Learn** button retains the original learning flow. Once `State.hasLearnedVerse` makes the button **Review**, tapping Review opens a **Learn** hub using the existing large Practice-card design: **Learn Again** (yellow, `app_icon_review_verse.png`) goes to the unchanged original Learn-the-Verse flow; **Listen** (purple, `app_icon_listen_to_verse.png`) opens a new **Listen Activities** list. Learn hub has the Practice-style Home pill. Listen Activities' Back pill returns to Learn.
- **Eight child-facing cards in exact owner order:** Balloons/yellow (`app_icon_balloons.png`), Fish/blue (`app_icon_fish.png`), Typewriter/purple (`app_icon_typewriter.png`), Keyboard/charcoal (`app_icon_keyboard.png`), Unscramble/green (`app_icon_unscramble.png`), Space/light gray (`app_icon_verse_crawl.png`), Tap in Order/purple (`app_icon_tap_words_in_order.png`), **Sound Sequence**/red (`app_icon_chunk_audio_matching.png`). Actual internal IDs stay `balloons,fish,typewriter,keyboard,unscramble,star_wars,tap_words_order,chunk_sequence`; the shortened labels do not mutate persisted IDs.
- **Safety-critical free play:** `read_my_verse.js` now admits a strictly validated, **child-only `learn_listen_activities` context** in addition to the existing real `daily_todo` context. It binds the active Zookeeper ID, current verse, enabled activity ID and normal (non-DEV) mode, and rejects a Daily plan/task/launch token, preview scope or wrong profile. `app.js` dispatches regular free-play completion/Back to **Listen Activities**, without `beginTask`, `setPendingCompletion`, Snack/To-Do credit or false rescued-pet odds; authentic Daily review assignment/completion/exit still uses its original, unchanged token and saved-state paths. Native audio activates from the genuine activity-card tap. Invalid/missing chunk choices display a child-friendly dialog; eligibility now observes the engine's 1–8 chunk launch limit.
- **Visual/platform behavior:** Reuses `practice-screen`, `practice-game-card`, scroll sizing, home/back pill and existing app colors; colors selected to keep text readable. New screens join normal slide routing and menu-arrival DOM retention. The PWA shell cache version was bumped and the ten uploaded icons added to required core paths for post-install offline access. No new external permission, Swift audio category, DEV dock or game reward rule.
- **Regression added:** `scripts/validate_learn_listen_activities.js` is called by `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`; checks uploaded files, card IDs/order/color/icon mapping, learned-vs-unlearned navigation, same-gesture native route, validated profile-bound free-play context, and absence of fake Daily awards, plus original Daily context rejection. **Source JS syntax check passed during authoring; full Node/Web/Swift GitHub Actions and actual iPhone sound are PENDING until the PR runs/reports.** CI is not proof of audible iPhone output.

- **Goal:** one production BibloZoo iOS app using the accepted native AVFoundation sound system, while preserving original iOS app identity/profiles, safe audio/microphone/session lifecycle, and working Web/PWA/Android behavior. No App Store release or `main` merge is part of this roadmap.

This is an **iPad orientation/multitasking compatibility warning** to review before distribution; it does not by itself prove any iPhone runtime failure. Don't change device/orientation product policy without owner approval.
- (2) In `BibloZooNativeSoundPlugin`, `AVAudioSessionInterruptionWasSuspendedKey` is deprecated as of iOS 14.5 (Apple recommends `AVAudioSessionInterruptionReasonKey`). Actual code `ios/App/App/BibloZooNativeSoundPlugin.swift`, `handleInterruption`, records the new `AVAudioSessionInterruptionReasonKey` but still uses the deprecated suspended key to distinguish a delayed suspension-history `.began` from a fresh takeover. Treat this as a **non-fatal warning that needs a focused compatibility/regression audit**, not a reason to delete or blindly replace recovery logic before physical audio-interruption testing. Source: https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfaudio/avaudiosessioninterruptionwassuspendedkey
