## 1. Product and users

BibloZoo helps children memorize Bible verses through guided learning, practice games, Playground activities, Flashcards, Read My Verse, daily activities, and collectible BibloPets. A parent/family can use multiple **Zookeeper profiles** on one device. The application is primarily static HTML/CSS/JavaScript, also available as a web/PWA experience and packaged on iOS with Capacitor.

Progress and profiles are kept locally on the device. Backup export/import is an explicit user action. Verify the live [privacy policy](../privacy_policy.html) and native privacy manifest before making any claim about data collection, accounts, tracking, or release readiness.

## 2. Non-negotiable preservation rules

1. **Audio ownership is activity-specific; the iOS target is changing.** Current Web-era recovery rules and actual-clock probes remain in effect in `ios-prep` and web/PWA/Android. In the *experimental iOS native-only migration*, do **not** require a Web Audio clock, create Web/HTML sound output as recovery, or interpret a bridge `prepared`/`accepted` result as human-audible proof. Build native, owner-scoped playback/interruption verification instead. Retain legitimate interruption protections and the existing `.soloAmbient` policy unless a separately designed, iPhone-tested change is approved. Read [AUDIO-RECOVERY.md](AUDIO-RECOVERY.md), noting that its legacy Web flow is not the target architecture for the experiment.
2. **Do not silently lose child progress.** Preserve active verse, score, round, timers, recordings, Playground work, and saved profile data across interruptions, exits, and migration. Respect normal versus special Daily Zoo To-Do navigation.
3. **No accidental rewards.** Daily Questions, Daily Zoo To-Do, Feeding, rescue statistics, and developer simulations have distinct eligibility rules. Do not award completion twice or give achievements for simulated failures.
4. **Interruptions and cancellation are real lifecycle events.** Clean up listeners, timers, pointer capture, audio handles, and overlays; stale asynchronous callbacks must not revive a completed/exited activity.
5. **One experience, different owners.** Shared Welcome Back / Zoo Escape controls presentation and rescue stages; Learn, Feeding, games, Playground, Flashcards, and Read My Verse still own their audio, pause/resume, and exit logic. Screens that never needed a prompt should remain unchanged.
6. **Accessibility and offline are first-class.** Retain tap-to-rescue alongside dragging, reduced-motion behavior, screen-reader guidance, and the asset/service-worker path needed by standalone games. Native Capacitor and web/PWA loading are not identical.
7. **No silent release of developer tools.** The hidden whole-app Zoo Escape test harness has historically been enabled, as has Feeding's separate test path. Check their *current source flags* and disable or gate them for public release; do not infer they were already disabled.
8. **Native / privacy changes require special review.** Do not casually alter Capacitor packaging, AVAudioSession category/activation rules, backup format, privacy policy, or `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy`.

## 3. Architecture — where to look

| Area | Current source of truth |
| --- | --- |
| Main app, title/home, Learn, navigation, local verse progress, backups | `index.html`, `app.js`, `styles.css` |
| Zookeeper profiles and profile-scoped data | `profiles.js`, `profiles.css` |
| Verse inventory and individual verse configuration | `verse_data/verse_list.json`, `verse_data/*.json` |
| 12 standalone practice games | `verse_games/registry.js`, `verse_games/<game>/index.html`, `game.js`, `game.css` |
| 5 Playground activities | `verse_playground/registry.js`, `verse_playground/<activity>/` |
| Shared game bridge, standalone lifecycle and Welcome Back | `verse_games/shared/bridge.js`, `welcome-back-return.js`, `welcome-back.js`, `welcome-back.css` |
| Zoo Escape stages, rescue stats, optional effect sounds and developer harness | `verse_games/shared/welcome-back-zoo-escape.js` |
| Web/native audio diagnostics and bounded recovery | `verse_games/shared/audio-diagnostics.js`, `welcome-back-clock.js`, `native-audio-recovery.js`, `audio-recovery-coordinator.js` |
| Native diagnostics/recovery bridges and scene lifecycle | `ios/App/App/AppDelegate.swift`, `ios/App/App/SceneDelegate.swift` |
| **Native audio experiment (PRs #8–#18 merged; iPhone gate still open)** | `ios/App/App/BibloZooNativeSoundPlugin.swift` (custom Capacitor/AVAudioPlayer plugin), `ios/App/App/SceneDelegate.swift` (registration), native Xcode resource memberships, `verse_games/shared/native-sound-smoke-test.js`, `verse_games/shared/feeding-native-audio-pilot.js`, `daily_questions_feed_game.js`, `scripts/validate_native_feeding_preview_c2.js` |
| Daily Questions and sessions | `daily_questions.js`, `daily_question_sessions.js`, `daily_question_generators.js`, `daily_questions.css` |
| Daily Zoo To-Do and completion UI | `daily_zoo_todo.js`, `daily_zoo_todo_ui.js`, `daily_zoo_todo.css` |
| Feeding Classic, Labyrinth, Melody, Bounce | `daily_questions_feed_game.js`, `daily_questions_feed_game.css` |
| Flashcards including microphone and effect handling | `flashcards/flashcards.js`, `flashcards/recording.js`, `flashcards/audio-effects.js` |
| Read My Verse playback/recording | `read_my_verse.js`, `read_my_verse.css` |
| Production bundle and native packaging | `scripts/build.js`, `capacitor.config.json`, `ios/`; experimental bundle ID also in `ios/App/App.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj`, display label in `ios/App/App/Info.plist` |
| Web/PWA offline cache | `service-worker.js` |
| Existing regression tests | `scripts/validate_*.js` and `package.json` scripts |
| Automatic PR and integration-branch checks (merged PR #3) | `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` |

The production build copies root runtime files and asset folders into ignored `dist/`; **the Capacitor sync does not replace `npm run build`**. Assets live in `profile_pictures/`, `pet_images/`, `verse_images/`, `verse_audio/`, `ui_audio/`, and `verse_fonts/`. Inspect `scripts/build.js` rather than assuming all source assets are bundled automatically.

## 4. Current behavior and important product decisions

- **Zoo Escape (72.1A–72.1D):** A failed Welcome Back audio-recovery attempt starts an escaped-pet rescue; a second failure can trigger ZOO BREAK with multiple pets; a final genuine failure exits safely to the title/home route. Pending native interruption is **deferred**, not counted as a failed attempt. The card remains playable without sound. The Zoo Escape developer menu tests failure/deferred cases without faking successful audio. Stage 3 rescue is not proof of restored audio—LOCK GATE still requires an activity-owned check.
- **Rescue achievements:** `BibloPets Rescued` and `Zoo Breaks Stopped` are per-Zookeeper; simulations must not increase them. The latter counts only after all Stage 3 animals are caught *and* real final-gate recovery succeeds. Stats belong in **Zookeeper Stats**, not as a persistent line beneath Zoo To-Do on the home screen. A genuine new achievement may trigger the one-time Zoo Hero home celebration.
- **Feeding (73–73.1):** Classic, Labyrinth, Melody, and Bounce use a two-step start: choose **Tilt** or **Drag**, then selected-method instructions and **Got It!**. The choice applies to that game session, including interruption recovery. Bounce re-serves do not reopen control choice. Collision notes and sizes have been tuned; Melody's visual contact should align with the scheduled musical note. Verify real iPhone behavior, especially motion permission and touch/tilt isolation.
- **Learn narration return gate ([PR #5](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/5), merged 2026-11-06):** Learn Listen/Chunks/Echo recovery now requires advancing HTML narration `currentTime` from the same source/attempt, not success of the unrelated UI Web Audio context. The 3.5-second bounded retry, interruption cleanup, and existing Zoo Escape owner callbacks remain. The final PR regression and web-build checks passed; physical iPhone verification has not been reported.
- **Verse Launch Game Menu pause ([PR #6](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/6), merged 2026-11-06; iPhone verification pending):** The word conveyor previously continued moving behind Game Menu. The fix stops and restarts it only when no menu/help/speed overlay or iOS Welcome Back owns pause, and freezes the asteroid bonus activity clock and delayed work behind menus. No audio-session or completion/reward changes. [Post-merge integration CI passed](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/42313008623); owner physical-device acceptance has not been reported.
- **Flashcards and Read My Verse:** These involve sensitive audio/recording state and must not auto-restart a recording or mistake an acknowledgement for audio recovery.
- **Read My Verse feedback-level audit ([PR #4](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/4), merged 2026-11-05):** [Detailed comparison](READ-MY-VERSE-FEEDBACK-LEVELS.md) found low synthesized peaks in Unscramble (0.075) and Tap Words in Order (0.072 correct / 0.034 wrong) versus about 0.238 for a comparable Tower of Bible correct-word voice. The PR increases positive peaks to 0.22 and Tap Words wrong to 0.11; typing sample levels receive a modest lift in Web Audio and HTML fallback. This is *not* narration or soundtrack gain normalization; device-level loudness and overlapping voices need owner testing.
- **PWA vs iOS:** `service-worker.js` is for the web/PWA; the native iOS app uses its bundled `dist/` content. Offline acceptance requires checking the relevant deployment, not just existence of a precache string.
- **Git line endings / native-audio experiment setup:** [PR #7](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/7) was merged on 2026-11-06 and normalized 20 JSON blobs plus Ghost Writer CSS to LF under existing `.gitattributes`; `scripts/validate_tracked_lf_endings.js` now guards that contract. Owner confirmed the Mac experiment worktree is clean at `c0a3b4bf` with `i/lf w/lf attr/text eol=lf`, and pushed `experiment/native-ios-audio` to the same commit.
- **Native-audio experiment identity ([PR #8](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/8), merged to `experiment/native-ios-audio`):** The merged setup changes the *experimental copy only*: Capacitor `appId=com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest` and `appName=BibloZoo Audio Test`, Xcode App target Debug/Release `PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER` to the same unique ID, and Info.plist `CFBundleDisplayName` to `BibloZoo Audio Test`. The existing development-team identifier, Info.plist microphone permission, AVAudioSession, app icon artwork, data formats, rewards, web runtime, and native audio logic are unchanged. This lets the test app coexist with original BibloZoo if signing and installation succeed. It does **not** clone families or saved profiles between app containers. Owner reported the two-app setup working on their Mac/iPhone. Continue to preserve/export backups, independently verify Xcode target signing and bundle ID, and do **not** interpret separate installation as proof of AVFoundation reliability. The experimental workflow watches PRs/pushes to `experiment/native-ios-audio`. PR #8 and the experiment branch's post-merge run passed Node and web build tests; neither result proves the native iOS app compiled or produced audible sound. See [TESTING.md](TESTING.md).

- **Native output PR B ([PR #10](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/10), merged into experiment; audible iPhone pilot with an unresolved status error):** A single fixed-ID MP3 test (`pilot-unlock` → existing `ui_audio/ui_pet_unlock.mp3`) is explicitly copied by Xcode's App resource phase and played from `Bundle.main` using `AVAudioPlayer`; a new Capacitor plugin is registered alongside, not in place of, existing audio diagnostics/recovery plugins. The on-screen **DEV · Native Audio** panel is shown only on native iOS test builds, **never auto-plays**, and needs a tap to preload/play. JS bounds timeouts and cancels stale background calls; Swift only allows foreground + `.soloAmbient`, filters IDs/volume, and stops on background/route changes/interruptions. **No Feeding, Learn, game audio or shared recovery is routed through native output yet.** A bridge `accepted:true` is *not* evidence of audible sound. Owner actually heard the clip in several tests; however, `interruption-pending` occurred on some taps, so a B.1 correction and explicit retest remain before PR C. See [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) and [AUDIO-RECOVERY.md](AUDIO-RECOVERY.md). Remove this developer UI before release.

These descriptions summarize the source and earlier project decisions; recheck the active code and tests for changes.

## 5. Build and testing

Read [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) for exact commands and manual device coverage. The normal Mac/iPhone loop after merging into `ios-prep` is:

```sh
# In GitHub Desktop, switch to ios-prep and Pull Origin first
npm ci                   # when dependencies or lockfile change / initial setup
npm run build
npx cap sync ios
npx cap open ios
# Then build/run the App target on the physical iPhone in Xcode
```

Run the relevant `npm run validate:...` and `node scripts/validate_*.js` suites for the changed systems. Run a broader regression selection before declaring a risky audio, storage, or daily-reward change safe. Existing `package.json` does not define one complete all-tests command. The initial `ios-prep` workflow adds two automated jobs—selected Node regressions and a production web build—on PRs targeting `ios-prep` and on pushes to `ios-prep`. Its included Flashcards Patch 69 validator was updated to verify versioned caching and offline asset inclusion without depending on the obsolete Patch 72.1 cache name. It does **not** compile the native Xcode target, deploy GitHub Pages, or cover every historical validator. The workflow was merged in [PR #3](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/3); check GitHub Actions for the **current PR commit's actual runs** before claiming new tests passed. CI/test pass and **owner's iPhone verification** are distinct results.

## 6. PR collaboration workflow (mandatory)

For **all app, game, native, documentation and regression work**, use feature branches and PRs. For the native-audio experiment, also follow the staged gates in [§10](#10-native-avfoundation-experiment-roadmap--living-experiment-only):

1. Re-read this handoff, then **refresh GitHub `ios-prep` HEAD**, open/merged PRs, issues, relevant source, `package.json`, and any available checks. Do not design from a stale chat or handoff.
2. Confirm the owner's latest requirement. Check whether their local edits have been **committed and pushed**; GitHub cannot see unpushed work. Normally **do not request a ZIP** when GitHub holds the correct source.
3. Create a short-lived feature branch from the current PR **target branch**—normally `ios-prep`, or `experiment/native-ios-audio` for the explicitly isolated native-audio workstream—never `main`. Keep changes focused, preserving the guardrails above.
4. Implement the code, focused regression tests, and **handoff update in the same branch/PR**. Update [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) or [AUDIO-RECOVERY.md](AUDIO-RECOVERY.md) when their contracts change.
5. Run what the environment actually supports; state commands, exact result and limitations truthfully. On GitHub, review any applicable Actions checks on the **final PR commit**, not an earlier one.
6. Open a PR targeting **`ios-prep` normally**, or **`experiment/native-ios-audio`** for the native-audio experiment. Prefer a draft while checks are incomplete; include the summary, risks, verification evidence, and a short iPhone checklist. **Leave it unmerged.** The owner reviews and merges.
7. After merge, the owner switches in GitHub Desktop to **the PR's target branch/worktree**, pulls, builds/syncs, and tests on a physical iPhone. If the device test fails, open a **follow-up PR** to that branch. An automated green check is not a claim of physical-device acceptance. Do not install experimental code using the original iPhone bundle identifier.
8. No direct pushes to `ios-prep` or `main`, PR merges, or releases without the owner's explicit instruction. Branches for each task, PR review, and owner-controlled merge are the default.

## 7. Mandatory handoff maintenance (self-perpetuating)

**Every future PR must update this file.** A future assistant reading this handoff must carry this instruction forward, not treat it as optional housekeeping:

- At the **start** of a task, compare this document with current `ios-prep`, recent commits, relevant PRs and tests; list any stale claims.
- **In the same PR as changes**, refresh the **last verified branch/commit and date**, update affected architecture/guardrail statements, note consequential decisions and test coverage, and revise the **Current work** table below. For a tiny change, a short accurate status/update is sufficient; do not append an endless patch-by-patch journal.
- Describe statuses precisely: **proposed**, **PR open**, **merged on `ios-prep`**, **automated checks passed**, **iPhone verified**, or **not yet verified**. Never turn one of these into another without evidence.
- Update [AUDIO-RECOVERY.md](AUDIO-RECOVERY.md) and [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) whenever applicable. Keep links and filenames accurate.
- Include the handoff in the PR's changed files and tick the corresponding [PR template](../.github/pull_request_template.md) box. If unable to update it, disclose the reason in the PR and flag it as unfinished for the owner; **do not silently skip maintenance**.
- Document only durable decisions and actionable open work. Correct stale content rather than preserving it as authority. The source, current tests and owner's latest directions always win.
- **At the end of each PR**, tell the owner what this document now says, what was actually verified, and what remains for the owner to test.

**Post-merge device findings are a second handoff trigger.** A PR can correctly document an open/pending owner gate at submission time, but once the owner merges and tests it, the next PR (or a docs-only correction if no code PR is appropriate) must record the **actual merge SHA, final CI status, owner-reported pass/fail details, outstanding exceptions, and next actionable PR**. Never leave a just-merged PR labeled “current/unmerged” as the newest status. If a known issue appears across games, record it as a reproducible observation without assigning a speculative root cause.

This policy is deliberately written *inside* the handoff so a completely new conversation can continue maintaining it without the old chat.

**Current PR and testing handoff — N7m Read PiP/AirPods external audio takeover:** Owner merged **N7l #81** into experiment HEAD `2663b2a0d416ef329334367ce8869406554012cb` ([merge-head two GitHub Actions jobs GREEN](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/43018077232)) and **tested pet unlock and original four-page onboarding native sounds successfully** (“working well”). This closes the reported N7l features for tested scope, not N7g global audio/privacy. **New reliable user issue:** while foreground in **Keyboard Read My Verse** with a paused YouTube PiP, tapping PiP **Play** starts YouTube, but BibloZoo does not pause/show the fixed Welcome Back popup and its audio becomes silent. AirPods **Play** starting Apple Music/MP3 while BibloZoo stays active has the same failure, and merely tapping the app does not reclaim audio. Source: Read's Swift plugin receives `AVAudioSession.interruptionNotification` `.began` but previously only sent clip-owned `biblozooReadOutput(kind:"interrupted")`; on quiet Keyboard/Typewriter, Order/Unscramble or Balloons/Fish/Crawl visual phases no clip exists, and UIKit `willResignActive` does not happen, so no Read activity callback. **N7m** emits an explicit Read **OS lifecycle event** `audioInterruptionBegan` on real `.began`, plus genuine route and media-services losses; shared Read native JS adapter invalidates old activation and forwards activity interruption even with **zero players**; existing `pauseReadForInterruption` freezes all active Read scenes/timers, shows same Welcome Back, no synthetic native `ended` or extra score. The deliberate **Let's go!** gesture still requests native `.soloAmbient` priority, but the old **quiet `ack`** and **animated no-narration** paths now keep Welcome Back until real native activation `activated:true` rather than immediately dismissing on a Promise; OS denial retains retry/Back. No automatic foreground audio activation, priority stealing on a mere tap, mixing/ducking, mic/capture, Web output fallback or changed UI. [N7m device acceptance](N7M-READ-EXTERNAL-AUDIO-WELCOME-BACK.md). **Source/CI is NOT evidence iOS emits `.began` in every specific AirPods/PiP hardware setup or iPhone audibility. N7m owner hardware PASS PENDING.**

**Last detailed interruption failure evidence is from PR #18 (before N1):** unchecked Web Classic sound worked; checked Native-only Classic produced **no audible sound**. The captured timeline already read `pending=YES began=1 ended=0 events=1 last=began reason=0` at `selected-native`, before the native prepare, and remained pending at `before-native`, `after-native` and `round-start`. Badge: `Native ONLY unavailable (interruption-pending); sounds SILENT`. The notification's actual origin is **unknown**: this is not proof that WebKit, Web Audio, AVAudioPlayer, a tap, or another app caused it. The DEV panel previously played native clips audibly, but a same-state, post-failure tap has not established whether it can play while this latch is pending. Neither successful game scoring nor a bridge's `accepted` response proves physical sound.

### Owner-approved architectural decision (supersedes the old hybrid Stage D–H roadmap)

- **Destination: iOS playback/output is native AVFoundation only.** BibloZoo stays a JavaScript/Capacitor application for screen UI, game logic, state, art, animation, and rewards. Do not reinterpret this as a full Swift game rewrite. Stable `ios-prep` is unchanged unless the owner later authorizes a separate integration PR.
- **In the isolated `.audiotest` iOS app, no silent fallback to Web Audio, HTMLAudioElement, or WebKit media output.** Once the native-only migration's global isolation gate exists, *every sound audible on that experimental iOS app must come through the native backend*. Preserve the old browser/PWA/Android implementations **for those platforms only**. Use original/stable BibloZoo separately for Web comparison, not covert playback inside the native experiment.
- **Temporary missing iOS sound is acceptable research debt, not release success.** Do not use Web sound to camouflage a missing native feature, and do not block progress solely because native tones, narration, or advanced effects have not yet migrated. Surface missing/native-failed state explicitly; preserve input, game progress, accessibility, exit, mute, rewards, and recording data. Before production integration all required output/recording flows must be covered or have an explicit reviewed policy.
- **`Got It!` should be the real user gesture for the selected native activity.** Remove the old Web Audio resume/clock precondition from the experimental iOS start/return path, and replace it with bounded native prepare/activation and appropriate native player/session evidence. Do not infer speaker audibility from `prepareToPlay`, `play()`, `isPlaying`, or `getDebugState`. A real interruption still needs a safe, explicit user action; do not auto-play on foreground.
- **Zoo Escape and ZOO BREAK are future FUN surprises, not audio-repair tools.** The existing Web-era failure-driven rescue behavior stays intact in stable/web paths during migration; the experiment can decouple it from audio failure. Design random, cooldown-aware opportunities on eligible `Got It!` taps only **after** dependable native playback and the new start lifecycle are established. No random rescue events in N1. See §10.
- **Protect each Zookeeper's data and gameplay.** No double completions, repeated rewards, lost verse/round/timer, interrupted recording replay, or simulated rescue credit. Experimental and stable iOS bundle IDs and storage remain separate; never overwrite the original installed app.
- **Separate output from capture/DSP.** Avoid WebKit-produced *audible output* on experimental iOS; microphone recording, Flashcards metering/Wacky Replay, and Read My Verse recording have different semantics and need an explicit, later native-input/DSP decision. Do not silently call the entire app fully native while those paths still use browser media APIs.

## 10. Native-only iOS architecture and incremental migration roadmap — owner-approved direction

**Purpose.** Replace the current mix of WebKit sound rendering, Web Audio recovery, and narrow native output with a single intentional AVFoundation sound system for the *experimental iOS app*. This incorporates the November 6 `Reliable iOS Audio for BibloZoo` assessment's Swift-plugin, native player, instrumentation and phased migration analysis, but **supersedes its Web fallback / hybrid pilot preference for this experiment**, per the owner's explicit decision on November 7. This is an engineering hypothesis with required physical-device validation, not a claim that AVFoundation prevents all interruptions.

**Scope:** experiments/feature PRs target **`experiment/native-ios-audio`**, branching from its current HEAD, not `main` or `ios-prep`; leave PRs for owner review/merge. Preserve the separate `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest` identity and the original BibloZoo installation. Update this handoff in every PR. Update [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) and [AUDIO-RECOVERY.md](AUDIO-RECOVERY.md) when actual code contracts change, including replacing old hybrid guidance with scoped native-only steps. Never delete working browser/PWA/Android audio code without explicit cross-platform review.

### Target architecture / boundaries

`JavaScript game/activity owners → one backend-neutral sound API → native Capacitor bridge → AVFoundation output (AVAudioPlayer for clips, AVAudioEngine when synthesized/polyphonic/timed sound warrants it) → iOS device route.` No independent Web/HTML output path should run inside the final experimental iOS configuration. Keep an allowlisted sound manifest, per-activity/request IDs and generations, and distinct `prepared` / `accepted` / `progressing` / **human heard** observations; never claim a native status API proves speaker output.

Native audio-session policy should have **one deliberate coordinator** for activation/interruption/route/media reset, not unrelated plugins racing to set category/active state. Begin by preserving `.soloAmbient`, silent-switch/foreground-only semantics, microphone privacy and the existing no-auto-replay rule. A category/activation redesign may be justified later **only** by a focused review and physical iPhone evidence. Stale `.began` notifications and genuine current interruptions must be distinguishable; never clear an actual pending interruption just to make a test appear green.

### N1 implementation and owner-reported isolation acceptance (not full native-audio success)

**N1 [PR #20](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/20), merged as `6303ce79`,** added [the complete audio output inventory](NATIVE-ONLY-IOS-AUDIO-INVENTORY.md), `verse_games/shared/native-only-ios-output.js`, build injection in `scripts/build.js`, `scripts/validate_native_only_ios_output.js`, experiment-scoped CI steps, and a native-only Classic tester interface. **GitHub Node regression and production web-build tests passed** in [run #42386368338](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/42386368338), including early script injection into **33 packaged HTML pages**. Revalidate checks for the final commit. **The early injection is only in generated `dist/`**, and only for the `.audiotest` app ID; browser/PWA/Android and original `ios-prep` output are not meant to change. `AudioContext`/`webkitAudioContext` are hidden on experimental iOS; HTML audio `play()` rejects instead of lying about success; video is muted; native Capacitor Swift plugin output is unaffected. Microphone `getUserMedia`/`MediaRecorder` APIs are untouched, **but Web Audio metering, recorded-take playback, Learn narration, UI sounds and unmigrated games may be silent or audio-gated**. Preserve recordings/state and report the limitations. The native-only Classic checkbox no longer offers a misleading Web choice in the built experiment.

**Owner iPhone test after merge:** in GitHub Desktop select `biblozoo-native-audio`, Fetch/Pull `experiment/native-ios-audio`, run `node scripts/validate_native_only_ios_output.js`, `npm run build`, `node scripts/validate_native_only_ios_output.js` again (verifies built pages), `npx cap sync ios`, `npx cap open ios`, then build/run **BibloZoo Audio Test** on a physical iPhone. Check that UI taps and nonmigrated sounds are silent without a Web fallback, native DEV Play star/chomp is audible (or capture rejection), Classic preview says Native ONLY, navigation/game exits remain usable, no mic auto-start occurs, and `interruption-pending` is recorded at each phase without guessing causality. The **original BibloZoo installation** remains the Web sound comparison. N1 does not compile Swift in Linux CI or demonstrate human audibility.

**N1 limitation to take into N2:** Blocking WebKit *output* does not eliminate every legacy HTML `new Audio()` and `load()` call, nor bypass actual iOS audio-session interruption signals. If DEV native output is still blocked in this isolated environment, focus N2 on one native session/lifecycle owner and a time-ordered, foreground-safe native interruption trace. Do not re-add a Web clock, Web fallback or speculative `setActive` loop.

### N3b–N3d sound foundations, N4a Melody, N4a.1 App Switcher and N4b Labyrinth owner passes, N4c Bounce gate

**Owner-provided on-device test evidence (2026-11-07):** Native N3b [PR #25](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/25) **merged as `29b424fa`** on `experiment/native-ios-audio`. Directly after Xcode build, the isolated iPhone test app's **FIRST LAUNCH HAD ABSOLUTELY NO SOUND**. Owner force-closed/restarted. Next **two trials succeeded** with audible native six-pitch Classic peg beeps (owner said they sounded fine), star/chomp, and both real task switches successfully recovered original gameplay/sound. Two copied native reports each contained exactly **80 retained events** (160), no event >=120ms software dispatch (most 1–2ms, total 0–3ms), worker queue wait 0ms. `feed-peg` had `avengine-tone` and `feed-chomp` `avengine-pcm`; prior N3a was also fast and audible. **Crucially, no first-failure diagnostic report was copied**, so the silence might have been a preflight rejection/timeout, never-activated initial session, Swift schedules without actual sound rendering, or another unobserved cause. Do not assert the failure's root cause or call post-restart success a first-launch pass. N3b in normal runs and two actual task-switch Resume paths did work. N3c was explicitly authorized; assistant chose a small focused PR rather than folding uncertain cold-launch repairs into N3 Melody migration.

**[PR #26](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/26) N3c — merged as `3ef3961e`:** The Swift plugin's `activateFromGesture` now requests bounded `AVAudioSession.setActive(true)` on the first real Got It!/Resume/DEV tap, while honoring `.soloAmbient`, real interruption latch and existing OS attempt/cooldown policy. Failed Native Classic `prepare()` stays at Instructions with **Try Again**/Back rather than starting a knowingly silent round. Existing one-tap DEV copy report appends last 18 fixed-ID native gesture/preload outcomes, in-memory only. Owner now physically tested first-tap startup, genuine mid-round task-switch and two YouTube PiP fresh launches. One PiP trial initially returned `preload feed-peg stale` but retried successfully, the next worked on first prep; exact stale origin unknown. No claim that a previous entirely silent first post-build launch can never recur.

**Failure UX:** When Native-only Classic `BibloZooFeedingNativeAudioPilot.prepare()` fails, `startPreviewRoundWithAudioChoice` retains **the existing Instructions screen** and **does not start a knowingly silent round**, returning `Try Again` button with readable allowlisted failure reason, DEV Copy Report hint, and Back; only a new user tap retries. No scoring/reward/round corruption, no fallback or automatic retry timers. On successful Swift preparation the regular Classic start stays unchanged. Even Swift `prepared` and `scheduleBuffer` success are **NOT proof of human hearing** and cannot detect a silent rendering engine.

**Diagnostics:** The same manual DEV **Copy Audio Timing Report** now appends **last 18 sanitized Swift native gesture and fixed sound-ID preload events** (phase/sound/reason/accepted/prep duration/foreground epoch/interruption pending/app active), after the original 80 PCM dispatch rows, and `getDebugState` shows first-gesture activation/last preflight. All in RAM, no child/verse/pet/recording/device data, no disk/network. **If first launch silent, copy report BEFORE quitting/restarting** so failure evidence survives; record whether the game was held at Try Again or had started silently. Audio status/preamble and Swift reason remain separate from ear-heard output.

**N3c owner gate actually exercised:** N3c was merged and physically tested after PR #26. Initial native `os-accepted`, three subsequent `prepared` outcomes and audible Classic with task-switch same-round return were reported. On **two separate fresh starts with YouTube PiP already running**, each final report contained 80 native scheduled events, no dispatch >=120ms; the first had stale peg preload then a second explicit prep that succeeded, and the other was successful on its first prep. PiP test `foreground epoch=0` is not a task switch. Keep the report/Retry facility to diagnose any future initial silence **before restarting**, rather than hiding an error.

### Standing release and evidence rules

1. **No hidden fallback:** If the experimental iOS branch produces sound, it must be traceable to a native sound ID and plugin call. Web/HTML paths are preserved for web/PWA/Android, not invoked to “save” native iOS output. Use the original installed BibloZoo app for any Web baseline.
2. **No false pass:** Silence is allowed **during development**, but is recorded as unmigrated/failed, never `audible native success`. Node mocks, successful `AVAudioPlayer.play()`, advancing clocks and Swift compilation are distinct from a human hearing the actual effect.
3. **Gameplay state is sacrosanct:** Preserve pet progress, verse, timers, recording/mic content, local backups, daily reward eligibility and accessibility. Interruption and cancellation must invalidate stale audio/game callbacks.
4. **Session safety and privacy:** No forced audio category change, recording activation, background entitlement, profile/verse/audio-content telemetry, or spontaneous resumed playback without dedicated review and device testing.
5. **PR discipline:** Each slice is a short-lived branch against `experiment/native-ios-audio`, with focused tests, mandatory update to this handoff (including stage status/date/commit/device evidence), relevant testing/audio docs, owner review and merge. Source/CI, Xcode build, and physical audible output always have separate statuses. Review hidden developer test menus before any public release.

**N7e implementation honesty detail:** Bridge `playCapture(started:true)` does **not** itself consume the child's one-use Wacky. A current-take/token `biblozooCapturePlayback(kind:started)` event is emitted only after nonzero post-DSP native mixer PCM, then the original `onStarted` consumes Wacky once; a separate `.dataPlayedBack` `kind:ended` completes the effect. Stop/route/inactive removes that render tap; it observes output only, never feeds microphone audio to speaker or JS. No source/CI event proves actual physical iPhone hearing.

Feeding `recover(info)` must NOT call `attemptFeedingResumeFromGesture` on selected cue; at final LOCK GATE it must invoke that existing owner within the actual tap, *never* treat cue-only verification as Feeding bank readiness or completed Daily snack credit.
